# Auto-Bake of AO and Curvature, with Masters That Survive UV Changes

**Issue ID:** N/A  
**Status:** Design (Session 1); hand-off for the first prototype (the master and the copy, AO only) written, not run.  
**Related Issues / Regressions:** wish GEN-032 in [../spec/general-tools.md](../spec/general-tools.md) and [20261010.fss-blender-tools-wishlist.md](20261010.fss-blender-tools-wishlist.md) (item 18, cockpit bake operator); tags Non-AO-Occluder (TAG-008) and Skip-AO-Bake (TAG-009) in [../spec/tags.md](../spec/tags.md) and [20261014.fss-blender-tools-tags-and-merge.md](20261014.fss-blender-tools-tags-and-merge.md) (Sessions 13 and 15); [20261019.automated-uv-unwrap.md](20261019.automated-uv-unwrap.md) (packing, Heatmap); [20261105.uv-seam-mismatch-substance.md](20261105.uv-seam-mismatch-substance.md) (seam padding on hold)

***

## Session 1: Design Discussion and Prototype Hand-off

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** 23e3083

### Objective

Bake AO, and later curvature, for cockpit parts without hand preparation, and keep the result usable after the UV
layout changes. Today the artist bakes AO by hand: the shared interaction parts (collection
`INTERACTIONS_SHARED_MESH`) are laid out on a plane and baked there, and for a panel's AO the asymmetric interaction
parts are hidden so they leave no irregular shadow. This takes a long time and has to be repeated after UV changes.

### Decisions (maintainer)

1. **A master of each map, outside the `.blend`,** like the `.visibility` file. It stays usable after UV islands are
   moved, rotated, scaled, re-unwrapped or moved to another texture set or texture size. A change of the UV layout is
   a copy from the master, never a re-bake.
2. **No hand preparation.** The auto-bake places no artificial occluders and needs nothing set up by the artist.
3. **AO, not a lightmap:** no airframe shell or other light blocker is added around the cockpit.
4. **Panels are baked with the buttons pressed,** the pose that shadows the panel least.
5. **The master is stored per island, not per face,** with many islands accepted and **no distortion at all**.
   Adjacent faces with the same normal join one island.
6. **The bake tools never change the artist's UV islands.** UVs come from the artist and the auto-packer. A density
   mismatch where two islands meet is not fixed by moving or reshaping islands.
7. **Sky light is useful,** but only for unique meshes: instanced copies share one mesh, one UV and one image.
8. **The first prototype is the master and the copy, AO only,** run as the hand-off below. The occluder automation
   follows after it.

### Proposals (not answered by the maintainer)

The hand-off follows P1 to P4 until the maintainer says otherwise.

- **P1: islands by unfolding.** An island grows from its largest free face. A neighbour across an edge joins by being
  hinged flat on that edge, so every face keeps its exact shape. A vertex may end up inside an island only when the
  face angles around it add up to 360° (flat, cylinders, cones), and the unfolded faces must not overlap. Coplanar
  joining (decision 5) is the simplest case; this rule also keeps knob sides and round button walls in one strip. The
  same mesh always gives the same islands.
- **P2: a skirt as the gutter.** Each island's border is surrounded by the neighbour faces, unfolded across the border
  edges into the gutter and baked as real surface, not by stretching border pixels. Pixels beyond a corner vertex
  (not 360°) take the nearest real face. Gutter width 2 px at master density. The skirt copy is invisible to rays so it
  casts no AO on the faces it duplicates.
- **P3: density and depth.** Master density twice the packing density, the same on every face. AO and curvature are
  one channel each, 16-bit integer (all AO is 16-bit, material decision S33.3), one image per object and per map;
  curvature is signed with 50 % grey as flat, as in Substance Painter.
- **P4: the copy into the artist's layout.** Every pixel of the target layout is sampled at several points; each point
  finds its triangle, maps through its barycentric coordinates into the master and reads it bilinearly. The sample count
  grows with the size ratio, so a small texture does not alias. The target's padding comes from extending the nearest
  triangle's mapping past its edge, which reads the real neighbour surface from the master (the island or its skirt).
  Faces whose target density is above the master's are reported.
- **P5: staleness.** Curvature is stale when the object's own mesh or normals change; AO also when an occluder within
  the AO distance, the occluder choice, the poses or the bake settings change. Separate files per map so one can be
  re-baked without the other.
- **P6: occluders without preparation.** Occluders are the real LOD0 scene minus Non-AO-Occluder objects and hidden
  kinds (collisions, clickspots, helpers, decals). Each surface is baked with moving parts in the pose that darkens it
  least: panels and bases with buttons pressed (the pose where the cap is deepest toward the panel, read from the
  animation, not from a frame number); caps released. A mover whose shape changes along its motion (pointer knobs,
  toggles, rockers, guards, the gear lever) is hidden for the panel; a round knob turning on its own axis stays an
  occluder. Shared meshes get one AO averaged over their copies; a low-resolution pre-pass measures how much the
  copies differ, and the artist chooses to keep the average, split into groups, or give them unique UV2.
- **P7: the plane for shared parts.** For a shared mesh, rays from its placed copies in their local ±X, ±Y, ±Z find the
  panel it sits on. A consistent nearest hit gives the down side and the plane height; disagreement is reported.
  Parts that sit together (cap with base, guard with guard base) are baked together on that plane.
- **P8: more maps later.** Ray-traced, with a master: sky light, cavity (AO with a short distance), thickness. Computed
  during the copy, with no master: ID map per object or merge group, world or object normal, position. Priority after
  AO and curvature: ID map, sky light, cavity.
- **P9: seams between islands of different density.** With the islands fixed (decision 6) only the sharpness can be
  evened out: near a seam, the copy reads with a footprint as large as the sparser side's pixels, fading back over a
  band. For AO this can be part of the copy; for painted maps it waits for the Substance seam investigation.

### Quality Analysis of the Copy Against a Direct Bake

A direct Cycles bake reads the true AO once per pixel, with Monte Carlo noise, and probably without anti-aliasing
(not checked). A copy reads it twice: into the master, then into the layout.

| Issue | Expected | Detection or remedy |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Softening from the second read | about half a target pixel at 2× master density | bicubic reading if needed |
| Aliasing | copy better: several samples per pixel | none needed |
| Noise | copy better, and the same in every re-layout | master needs enough samples once |
| Target denser than master (scaled-up islands, larger texture, stretched unwrap) | blur where a direct bake has detail | reported per face; re-bake that object's master denser |
| Target much coarser than master | aliasing if samples are too few | sample count grows with the ratio; no master mips (a 2 px gutter vanishes at the second mip) |
| Island borders in the master | exact with the skirt, except a point-sized wedge at non-flat corners | nearest real face there |
| Target padding | equal to Blender's Adjacent Faces margin | extended mapping (P4) |
| Normals and settings | a wrong master with no visible sign if they differ | staleness covers normals, AO distance, samples, poses |
| Stacked target faces | the same as a direct bake | average or one face wins |
| Bugs: half-pixel offset, drift along long strips, skirt casting AO | obvious errors | the prototype tests each |

Expected: equal to a direct bake or better in aliasing and noise, slightly softer, worse only where the target is
denser than the master, which is detected.

### The Cockpit as It Is (E2 `COCKPIT.blend`, copy in `tests/_work/ao_bake/`, read only)

- 4,050 objects. `INTERACTIONS_SHARED_MESH`: 55 mesh objects, 54 meshes, 52 of them shared; one reference copy of
  each on a 5 cm grid at Z 0, unrotated, without parent, local Z up.
- 800 placed copies, 583 moving, mostly through parent empties (`EMPTY_*`, `@NOMERGE_*`). Placed copies have their
  local Z along the panel normal (breakers rotated −81.6° about Y, pedestal buttons −8° about X, the ADS heater −70°).
- Most used: `CIRCUIT_BREAKER_BASE` and `CIRCUIT_BREAKER_CAP` 115 users each (bases static, caps all moving),
  `BUTTON_SQUARE` 61, `BTN_BASE` and `BTN_CAP` 53, `BUTTON_MCDU_MENU` 44.
- Most meshes start at local Z 0; bases go below it (`BTN_BASE` −6.73 to 0 mm, `BTN_GUARD` −5.41 to 1.45 mm,
  `TOGGLE1_BASE` −5.16 to 1.70 mm). Exceptions: `ROTARY_CAP2` and `ROTARY_CAP3` start at 1.65 mm, `ROTARY_FGC_CAP1`
  −11 to 13 mm, `FGC_CAP2` −10.5 to 10.45 mm, `FGC_WHEEL` −5 to 5 mm, `LEVER_LANDING_GEAR` −19.5 to 49.3 mm (UV map
  named `Baker`). So "local Z 0 is the panel" holds for most meshes, not all, which is why P7 measures it.
- No screws in this collection. Material mostly `MAT_STD_COCKPIT_INTERACTION`.

### Open Questions for the Maintainer

- Own AO of toggles and levers: averaged over poses, or the rest pose.
- Where the screws are kept, and whether one averaged AO is acceptable for them.
- The AO distance the artists use in the manual bake (the authoring guide gives none). The prototype uses 0.1 m.
- Whether a manual bake of the interaction parts exists to compare the occluder automation against later.
- Whether artists bake normal maps from high-poly models (a normal-map master would need object-space storage).

### Hand-off: Prototype of the Master and the Copy (AO Only)

#### Read First

- [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md) (Testing, Pitfalls Learned) and the global CPU rule below.
- This session: decisions 1, 5, 6 and 8, proposals P1 to P4, and the quality analysis. They are the specification.
- `F:\new-fss-aircraft-ejet\doc\msfs-authoring\baking.md` (AO in MSFS, 16-bit, test at 128 px).
- `fss_blender_tools/visibility_core.py` around `load_visibility_file` and `VISIBILITY_SCHEMA`, only as an example of
  an external npz file with metadata. The prototype does not use or change it.

#### Rules for Running Anything

- **CPU:** at most 12 of the 24 logical cores, at below-normal priority, every Blender run started from **PowerShell**
  like this (never from Git Bash, which is banned on this machine):

  ```powershell
  cmd /c 'start "" /wait /b /belownormal /affinity FFF G:\e-jets-e2\tools\blender\blender.exe -b --factory-startup <blend> --python <script>'
  ```

- Cycles on the CPU with at most 12 threads (`scene.render.threads_mode = 'FIXED'`, `threads = 12`); the GPU is in use
  by another VM.
- One Blender at a time (`tasklist | findstr /i blender` before starting). Never two runs, or a run and a build, at once.
- Scripts write their results to files and print progress; start with the smallest sizes (128 px, few samples) and
  time a run before going larger. A run that takes over 10 minutes is stopped and reported, not waited out.

#### Steps

1. **Set up.** Scripts in `tests/bake_prototype/`, output in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` (git-ignored). Use the existing
   copy `tests/_work/ao_bake/COCKPIT.blend`; save every changed scene as a new file there, never over the copy. Pick
   three objects and record their names:
   - a small flat panel with some `BTN_CAP` / `BTN_BASE` copies on it (the buttons are occluders only);
   - one `BTN_CAP` copy;
   - one rotary knob copy (a cylinder side, to test P1's unrolling).

   Occluders for every bake: all visible mesh objects whose bounding box lies within the AO distance (0.1 m) of the
   target's bounding box, in the file's current pose. No pose logic, no tags, no pre-pass in this prototype.
2. **Check the ray-invisible copy first.** Bake AO of one object directly into its own UV, then bake a coincident copy
   of it (same mesh and split normals, ray visibility off, the original left visible as occluder) into the same UV.
   They must agree within the noise of two direct bakes with different seeds. If they do not, stop and report: P2
   depends on it.
3. **Build the master** (P1, P2, P3) as a separate bake mesh: the islands by unfolding, each with its skirt, laid out
   at 2× the target's median density, packed with 2 px gutters, split normals copied from the original. Check: every
   face's master edge lengths equal its 3D edge lengths times the density (relative error at most 1e-4); no overlaps;
   the island count and the gutter's share of the master's pixels.
4. **Bake the master's AO** in Cycles into a 16-bit single-channel image; fill the wedges beyond corners from the
   nearest real face; save as 16-bit PNG with the master UV per face corner and the density in an npz beside it.
5. **Make the target layouts** on copies of the object's UV map (the map its material uses), at the real texture size
   of its material's images, or 4096 if none is found:
   - (a) unchanged;
   - (b) every island rotated 37° about its centre, then packed without scaling;
   - (c) islands scaled 0.5×; (d) scaled 1.9× (still below master density); (e) scaled 2.5× (above master density);
   - (f) a re-unwrap with different seams (Smart UV Project), scaled to the same median density;
   - (g) layout (a) at a quarter of the texture size.
6. **Copy** the master into each layout (P4), with padding of 8 px.
7. **Bake references** directly into each layout: ground truth with many samples (at least 8× the normal count), and
   a normal bake with the same samples as the master. Note whether Cycles bakes anti-aliased (jittered inside the pixel)
   or at the pixel centre only.
8. **Measure** against ground truth, over pixels inside triangles, for the copy and for the normal direct bake: mean
   absolute error, RMS, 99th percentile, maximum; the same in a 2 px band along the target's seams; and in the padding.
   Save the images and difference images as PNG for review. Record the master's disk size, the bake and copy times.
9. **Append Session 2** to this journal: the objects, the numbers per object and layout, each finish line as MET or NOT
   MET, what failed and why. Commit only the prototype scripts and this journal, by name (the working tree holds
   other people's uncommitted changes), with a conventional message, without pushing.

#### Finish Line

Report each as MET or NOT MET, with numbers.

1. **Ray-invisible copy:** step 2 agrees within the noise of two direct bakes.
2. **No distortion:** step 3's edge-length check passes for every face of the three objects; no overlaps; the knob's
   side is one island.
3. **Copy as good as a direct bake:** for layouts (a), (b), (c), (d), (f) and (g), the copy's mean absolute error and
   99th percentile against ground truth are no larger than the normal direct bake's.
4. **No seam lines:** in the seam band the copy's mean absolute error is at most 1.5× its error inside islands, and no
   line is visible in the difference images.
5. **Denser than master is detected:** in layout (e) the faces above master density are listed with their count, and
   the error there is reported.
6. **Cost:** master size per object, the gutter's share, and the times are reported.

#### Do Not

- Do not change `fss_blender_tools`, the exporter or any shipped add-on; this is a prototype in `tests/` only.
- Do not open, save or export into the production `COCKPIT.blend` or any aircraft repository folder.
- Do not bake the whole cockpit, curvature, or anything beyond the three objects; do not build the occluder automation.
- Do not use Git Bash, and do not run Blender or Cycles without the CPU wrapper.
- Do not loosen a criterion or pass it "with an explanation": report it NOT MET with the numbers.
- Do not push and do not deploy.

## Session 2: Prototype of the Master and the Copy (AO Only)

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar
- **Agent:** Claude Code (Opus 5.5), sub-agent executing the hand-off of Session 1
- **Branch:** main
- **Base Commit SHA:** 23e3083

The hand-off of Session 1 was run on the cockpit copy `tests/_work/ao_bake/COCKPIT.blend`, with four rules the
maintainer added during the run (below). Scripts in `tests/bake_prototype/`, outputs in `tests/_work/ao_bake/`
(git-ignored). Every Blender run: the CPU wrapper (affinity FFF, below-normal), `--factory-startup`, Cycles CPU with
12 fixed threads, one Blender at a time. No run came near 10 minutes (the longest, all seven layouts of the cap, took
about 3 minutes). Nothing in `fss_blender_tools`, the exporter or any aircraft repository was changed.

### Maintainer Rules Added During the Run

1. Use the UV maps already on the objects; the master's layout lives only on the separate bake mesh, the test layouts
   only on copies of the UV map.
2. No decals (and no glass, invisible or occluder material, `uv_autoseam._is_skip_material`) as targets or occluders;
   such faces on mixed objects are not baked.
3. Targets only where `tags.is_ao_baked` allows, occluders only where `tags.is_ao_occluder` allows.
4. Record per target and occluder whether it is animated (tag mark `fss_animated_tags`, or an action, NLA strip or
   driver on the object or a parent); prefer static targets.

### Objects and Rules Applied (Step 1)

`extract_scene.py` writes each target with its occluders into `scene_<tag>.blend` (a collection; writing a whole
scene crashed Blender 5.2.2 in `libraries.write`): the evaluated target (current pose, modifiers applied) rebuilt from
Blender's own loop triangles with its split normals (largest change 0.08 degrees) and UV maps, the occluders evaluated,
all moved so the target's box centre is the origin. The rules are copied in `bake_rules.py` (registered properties are
read with `bl_system_properties_get()`; the tag reading was verified: the scene's `fss_invisible_tags` reads
`CLICKSPOT;COLLIDERS`).

| Tag | Object | Mesh | Faces (triangles) | UV map, texture | Animated |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | ---: | :--- | :--- |
| panel | `INHIBIT_PANEL` | `MESH_INHIBIT_PANEL`, 4 BTN_CAP and 4 BTN_BASE copies on it | 632 | `UVMap`, 4096 (no image in the material: default) | no |
| cap | `GND_PROX_INHIB_L_BTN` | `MESH_SHARED_BTN_CAP` (BUTTON_CAP), on the panel | 446 | `UVMap`, 4096 (default) | yes, through `@NOMERGE_GND_PROX_INHIB_L_BTN`; every BTN_CAP copy on the panel is animated |
| knob | `ROTARY_IESS_BARO` | `MESH_SHARED_ROTARY_CAP_IESS`, 20-sided side | 218 | `UVMap`, 4096 (default) | yes, through `EMPTY_IESS_BARO_KNOB_BASE` |

What each rule excluded:

| | Visible scene (2681 mesh objects, reference copies of `INTERACTIONS_SHARED_MESH` left out) | panel | cap | knob |
| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Objects within 0.1 m (boxes) | | 116 | 93 | 46 |
| Only skip materials (decal, glass, invisible, occluder): not target, not occluder | 644 | 34 | 27 | 14 |
| Some skip-material faces (dropped from the bake meshes) | 84 objects | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Skip-AO-Bake or No Texture tag (not a target) | 0 (no tag marked in this file) | target allowed | target allowed | target allowed |
| Non-AO-Occluder tag (not an occluder) | 0 (no tag marked in this file) | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Occluders used | | 82 (62757 tris) | 66 (54775 tris) | 32 (67297 tris) |
| Occluders animated (none tagged; all through an action on themselves or a parent) | 1235 objects animated | 27 | 20 | 8 |

The file's object tags are CLICKSPOT (241), GLASS (10), GAUGE (13), LIT (9) and COLLIDERS (5); none of them is listed
in `fss_ao_no_occluder_tags`, `fss_ao_skip_bake_tags`, `fss_no_texture_tags` or `fss_animated_tags` (all empty). Five
knob occluders carry GAUGE (`LCD_DU2`, `LCD_DU3`, `LCD_IESS_SCREEN`) or LIT (`LT_FLOOD_L`, `LT_FLOOD_R`); they occlude.
Animated occluders stay in the file's current pose (lists in `scene_<tag>.json`).

### Step 2: The Ray-Invisible Copy

`step2_invisible_copy.py`, panel, layout (a) at 4096 (crop 303 x 204 px, 18642 pixels), 64 samples, margin 0. A
variant passes when, against two direct bakes D1 and D2 (seeds 1, 2), its mean |d| and RMS are at most 1.1 x those of
D1 against D2, its |bias| at most 0.1 x that mean |d|, and no pixel is missing.

| Variant (coincident copy, seed 3, target visible) | mean \|d\| vs D1 / D2 | RMS vs D1 / D2 | Bias vs D1 | Result |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | ---: | :--- |
| D1 against D2 (the noise) | 0.0207 | 0.0276 | -0.0000 | reference |
| Every ray visibility off (as the hand-off says) | 0.6887 / 0.6887 | 0.7573 / 0.7574 | -0.6887 | NOT MET: Cycles drops an object with no visibility at all, the bake writes 0 |
| Every ray visibility off but camera | 0.3493 / 0.3493 | 0.4045 / 0.4046 | -0.3486 | NOT MET: AO rays hit the coincident target at distance 0 |
| As above, moved 1e-6 m along the vertex normals | 0.0206 / 0.0205 | 0.0276 / 0.0273 | +0.0002 | MET |
| As above, 1e-5 m | 0.0206 / 0.0205 | 0.0276 / 0.0273 | +0.0003 | MET |
| As above, 1e-4 m | 0.0207 / 0.0206 | 0.0277 / 0.0275 | +0.0013 | MET |
| Every visibility off, baked Selected to Active from the target | 0.0207 / 0.0206 | 0.0278 / 0.0276 | +0.0001 | MET |

The copy as specified (coincident, ray visibility off) does not work in Cycles. Selected to Active works without any
offset: Cycles evaluates the AO on the target's own triangle at the copy's point, with the target's self-intersection
exclusion. The master (core and skirt) is therefore baked Selected to Active from the target (extrusion 2e-5 m, ray
distance 4e-5 m), with every ray visibility of the bake meshes off. An emission bake of a 5 mm sine of the position
through master and copy (`check_mapping.py`) agreed with the direct bake to MAE 0.012 (softening only), so the
projection lands on the right faces.

Anti-aliasing: an emission bake of a sharp step gives 0 of 18642 pixels between 0 and 1 with 1 sample and 74 with
64 samples, so Cycles jitters inside the pixel (sample 0 at the centre). The copy matched GT only after its points
were kept on the pixel's own triangle (below), which fits Cycles keeping its jitter on that triangle; the Cycles source
was not read to confirm it.

### Steps 3 and 4: The Master

`step3_master.py` with `bake_unfold.py`: density = 2 x the area-weighted median density of layout (a); islands by
unfolding; skirt; shelf packing with each island's rectangle 2 px larger on every side; checks; bake of core and skirt
(64 samples, AO distance 0.1 m); compose (core pixels, then skirt pixels, then the rest of each island's rectangle from
the nearest baked pixel of the same island); 16-bit PNG and an npz (master UV per face corner in float64, island per
face, density, rectangles, face map, settings, mesh fingerprint).

P1 as implemented, and what changed on the way (an agent's choice, for the maintainer to confirm):

- A vertex may get two positions in an island; it is then on the border at both (the cut of an unrolled cylinder). The
  first implementation allowed one position per vertex and island; it could not close a cylinder's strip (the last
  quad was refused) and split the knob's side into 2 or more islands. P1's 360 degree rule still holds: a vertex is
  inside an island only when its fan closes.
- Join order: an island grows from the largest free triangle, the neighbour across the longest shared edge first.
  Smallest fold first (the other natural reading) hinges the knob's 20 side faces one by one onto its 15 degree bevel
  ring like petals (21 of 40 side edges continuous, the side in one island only formally); longest edge first unrolls
  the side as one strip (39 of 40 continuous, one cut). No fold limit is needed.
- Overlap is tested on triangles shrunk by 1e-6 about their centres (separating axes), so shared edges do not count.

| | panel | cap | knob |
| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Layout (a) median density, px/m | 1368.0 | 15642.9 | 10050.5 |
| Master density, px/m | 2736.1 | 31285.9 | 20101.0 |
| Master size, px | 547 x 273 | 995 x 887 | 843 x 488 |
| Islands | 23 | 14 | 1 |
| Joins refused (overlap) | 216 | 117 | 0 |
| Border edges (without a 3D neighbour) | 640 (24) | 466 (40) | 220 (20) |
| Skirt pieces | 975 | 852 | 400 |
| Edge length error, max relative (float64 / float32 UV) | 1.1e-13 / 2.6e-5 | 2.4e-14 / 6.4e-6 | 8.1e-15 / 3.8e-6 |
| Faces over 1e-4 | 0 of 632 | 0 of 446 | 0 of 218 |
| Pixels covered by two core triangles | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Share of master pixels: islands / gutter (skirt + corner fill) / unused | 52.6 % / 17.7 % / 29.7 % | 67.6 % / 5.5 % / 26.9 % | 65.2 % / 5.6 % / 29.3 % |
| Bake core / skirt, s | 0.50 / 0.07 | 4.16 / 0.26 | 2.18 / 0.14 |
| Build (islands, skirt, pack), s | 0.29 | 0.20 | 0.09 |
| On disk: PNG / npz, kB | 56.8 / 30.7 | 364.7 / 51.7 | 125.4 / 25.9 |

Walls around the local Z axis (faces whose normal is within 0.2 of perpendicular, connected, going all the way
round): the knob's side, 40 triangles, is one island with 39 of 40 inner edges continuous. The cap has two such walls
in one island each (76 of 80) and one split over 11 islands; the panel's walls (button holes) are in 2 or 3 islands.

### Steps 5 to 8: Layouts, Copy, References, Measurement

`step5_layouts.py` makes the layouts on copies of `UVMap` in the extracted scene (the object's own map is not
changed): (a) unchanged; (b) every UV island rotated 37 degrees about its box centre; (c), (d), (e) islands scaled 0.5,
1.9, 2.5 about their centres; (f) Smart UV Project (66 degrees) on a duplicate, scaled to (a)'s median density; (b) to
(f) shelf-packed without rotation or scaling, islands 16 px apart; (g) layout (a) at 1024. Every layout is baked into
a crop of its texture (the pixel grid unchanged). GT: direct bake, 1024 samples (16 x); direct: 64 samples (the
master's count); both with an 8 px Adjacent Faces margin. Copy: `bake_copy.py`, 8 px padding.

The copy (P4) as measured:

- Each pixel whose centre is in a triangle is sampled on an L x L grid, L per triangle = 2 x the pixel's footprint in
  master pixels (largest singular value of the layout-to-master map), at least 2, at most 16; no pixel reached 16
  except in layout (g) of the panel (816 pixels).
- Only grid points on the pixel's own triangle count, because a direct Cycles bake keeps its jitter on that triangle.
  Integrating over the whole pixel (the first version) was further from GT: panel (a) P99 0.077 against 0.048, (g)
  MAE 0.027 against 0.008.
- The master is read bilinearly, but only from texels that show a face within 30 degrees of the sampled face (the
  face map of the master); without this the reads across sharp folds blurred AO from the other face into the edge
  pixels: panel (a) P99 0.048 without, 0.033 with; seam MAE 0.0118 without, 0.0084 with.
- A master point outside its island's baked pixels is clamped into its triangle.
- Padding: the nearest triangle, its nearest edge, the 3D neighbour across that edge hinged flat into the layout,
  read from the master. Cycles' margin writes 0 into many padding pixels (panel (a): see below), so the padding is
  compared only where GT is above 0.

noise; the copy's master has the same sample count but each layout pixel averages several master pixels. Layout (d)
was meant to stay below master density; because the artists' UVs vary in density, 79 panel faces and 142 cap faces
are above it already at 1.9x. The panel's copy has single outlier pixels (max 0.40 in (a), 0.53 in (d), against 0.13
and 0.16 for the direct bake), along the thin frame strips.

Padding: the copy reads the real neighbour surface, Cycles' Adjacent Faces margin does not. On the panel the worst padding pixels lie beyond edges without a 3D
neighbour (the mesh's open border; 24 such edges on the panel, 40 on the cap): Cycles writes 0.01 to 0.09 there, the
copy reads the face itself, 0.76 to 0.90 (inspect_padding.py; the cap was not inspected pixel by pixel). The quality analysis row "target padding equal to
Blender's Adjacent Faces margin" is therefore not confirmed: copy against GT in the padding 0.10 to 0.30 on the panel
and the cap, 0.01 to 0.04 on the knob. Which of the two is better for the simulator was not judged.

| No. | Criterion | Result |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Ray-invisible copy agrees within the noise of two direct bakes | NOT MET as specified: every ray visibility off gives 0 (mean \|d\| 0.689 against the noise 0.021), camera-only gives 0.349. The Selected to Active variant is MET (0.0207 / 0.0206, RMS 0.0278 / 0.0276 against 0.0276, bias +0.0001) and is what the master uses |
| 2 | No distortion: edge lengths, no overlaps, knob side one island | MET: largest relative edge error 1.1e-13 (float64; 2.6e-5 with float32 UVs), 0 of 1296 faces over 1e-4; 0 pixels in two core triangles; the knob's side (40 triangles) is one island, 39 of 40 inner edges continuous |
| 3 | Copy MAE and P99 no larger than the direct bake's, layouts a, b, c, d, f, g | MET: 18 of 18 (largest copy/direct: panel (d) MAE 0.0089 / 0.0149, P99 0.0394 / 0.0527; panel (g) P99 0.0384 / 0.0566). Outside the criterion, panel (e) P99 0.0524 / 0.0508 |
| 4 | Seam band MAE at most 1.5 x interior, no line visible | NOT MET: panel (c) 1.58, (d) 1.69, (e) 2.12, (f) 1.71; lines visible in the panel's (d) and (f) difference images along the thin frame strips. Cap 0.75 to 0.82 and knob 0.76 to 1.37 MET, no line visible in their images |
| 5 | Layout (e): faces above master density listed with count, error reported | MET: panel 572 of 632 faces (99.0 % of the area), MAE copy / direct 0.0101 / 0.0147, P99 0.0522 / 0.0508; cap 215 of 446 (50.9 %), 0.0131 / 0.0225, P99 0.0454 / 0.0762; knob 218 of 218, 0.0085 / 0.0146, P99 0.0335 / 0.0527 (per-face lists in `results.json`) |
| 6 | Cost reported | MET: master size, shares and times in the master table; bake and copy times in the result tables |

Why the panel's seams fail (an assumption, not measured face by face): the panel's frame has strips and bevels about
1 to 2 master pixels wide; the surface-aware read then finds few texels of the right face and falls back to texels of
the neighbour face. A denser master for such faces, or a minimum master width per face, would be the next thing to try.

### Open Points for the Maintainer

- Step 2's specified variant does not work in Cycles; the master is baked Selected to Active instead (no offset).
- P1: one position per vertex and island could not close a cylinder; the implementation allows a border vertex twice
  (the 360 degree rule still holds), and grows islands by the longest shared edge. Both are agent choices.
- P4: three refinements over plain bilinear reading were needed to match a direct bake: sample count per face from its
  footprint, only points on the pixel's own triangle, and reading only texels of the same surface (30 degrees).
- The padding does not match Cycles' margin at open borders (above).
- The density of 2 x is too low for faces narrower than about 2 master pixels (panel seams).

### Files

`tests/bake_prototype/`: `bake_common.py` (paths, PNG, Cycles bake), `bake_rules.py` (maintainer rules),
`bake_geom.py` (rasterizing, islands of a UV map), `bake_unfold.py` (P1, P2, packing, checks), `bake_copy.py` (P4),
`extract_scene.py` (step 1), `step2_invisible_copy.py`, `step3_master.py` (steps 3, 4), `step5_layouts.py`
(steps 5 to 8), `check_mapping.py` (emission check), and inspection helpers (`inspect_*.py`). Outputs in
`tests/_work/ao_bake/` (`scene_*`, `step2/`, `master/`, `layouts/<tag>/`; `layouts/panel_k`, `panel_tri` and
`panel_surf` hold the comparison runs of the copy refinements).

## Session 3: Review of Session 2, and Maintainer Answers

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** f9f9db6

### Maintainer Answers to Session 1's Open Questions

- **No high-poly models:** a normal-map master (P8) is dropped.
- **No manual bake exists.** A reference bake for the occluder automation has to be made first, before that
  automation is compared against anything.

### What Was Re-Checked

- **Commit f9f9db6** holds only the prototype scripts, this journal and the index row; the other uncommitted changes in
  the working tree were not staged.
- **Re-run:** `step5_layouts.py -- tag=panel samples=64 gt_samples=1024 layouts=adf suffix=_review` under the CPU
  wrapper (12 s). Layouts (a), (d) and (f) of the panel reproduce Session 2's numbers exactly: MAE copy / direct
  0.0064 / 0.0152, 0.0089 / 0.0149, 0.0065 / 0.0149; seam / interior 1.49, 1.69, 1.71; maximum 0.397, 0.530, 0.168.
  Cap and knob were not re-run.
- **Images:** `layouts/panel/d_inside_diff_x8.png` shows the lines along the panel's frame strips in the copy and not
  in the direct bake, as reported. `master/master_panel.png` shows the panel as one plain island with the darkening
  around the four button openings, and the frame's strips as thin islands along its edges.

### Findings

1. **Session 2's finish lines stand as reported:** 2, 3, 5 and 6 MET; 1 NOT MET as specified (Selected to Active
   works instead); 4 NOT MET on the panel.
2. **Outlier pixels are not covered by any finish line.** The panel's copy has single pixels up to 0.40 (a) and 0.53
   (d) off, against 0.13 and 0.16 for the direct bake, on the same frame strips as the seam lines. A speck of 0.5 is
   visible in a texture, so the next finish line must bound the maximum too, not only MAE and P99.
3. **The padding comparison has no ground truth.** GT's padding is Cycles' own margin, so "copy against GT in the
   padding" measures the difference from Cycles' convention, not an error. At open mesh borders Cycles writes dark
   values (0.01 to 0.09) and the copy repeats the face (0.76 to 0.90). A dark rim at an open border darkens the edge
   pixels in the smaller mips, so the copy's choice is probably the better one; not checked in the simulator.
4. **The copy is better than a normal direct bake because it averages more:** each layout pixel reads several master
   pixels, so the 64-sample noise averages down. That is what the criterion asked for, and the GT is an independent
   1024-sample direct bake, so the comparison is not circular.

### Proposals (not answered by the maintainer)

- **P10: accept Selected to Active for the master bake** in place of the ray-invisible copy (Session 2, step 2).
- **P11: accept Session 2's choices in P1 and P4:** a border vertex may appear twice in an island; islands grow by the
  longest shared edge; the copy samples per face footprint, keeps points on the pixel's own triangle, and reads only
  texels of the same surface (within 30 degrees).
- **P12: a minimum width per face in the master.** Faces narrower than a few master pixels (the panel's frame strips)
  get a locally higher density, or their own island at a higher density, so the surface-aware read always finds
  enough texels of the right face. Then re-measure finish line 4 and a new maximum-error line on the panel.
- **P13: the copy's padding repeats the face at open mesh borders,** instead of Cycles' dark margin.

### Hand-off: P12, a Minimum Face Width in the Master

The maintainer asked to try P12 next (20261112). Executed by a Claude sub-agent, which appends Session 4 and commits
by name without pushing.

**Read first:** Session 2 (results, finish lines), the findings above, then `tests/bake_prototype/bake_unfold.py`,
`step3_master.py`, `bake_copy.py` and `step5_layouts.py`.

**Rules for running anything:**

- PowerShell only, never Git Bash.
- One Blender at a time (`tasklist | findstr /i blender` before each run). Start Blender through the CPU cap, never
  with `/affinity`: `C:\Users\uray\.claude\tools\cpucap\cpucap.exe 50 G:\e-jets-e2\tools\blender\blender.exe -b
  --factory-startup <blend> --python <script> -- <args>`, and Cycles at 12 threads or fewer.
- Work only on the copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/`; the production `COCKPIT.blend` stays untouched.
- The maintainer's bake rules hold: always use the UV map that is already there, never bake decal (or other skip)
  materials, read the tags that allow an object to be baked or to occlude, and check whether an object is movable.

**Steps:**

1. **Measure before changing anything.** For the panel, give every face its master width (the smallest altitude of
   its triangles in master pixels) and find the faces under the pixels where the copy is wrong in layouts (a) and (d)
   (error above 0.1, and the seam band). Report how many of those pixels lie on faces narrower than 2, 3 and 4
   master pixels. If most of them do not, stop here and report: the cause is something else.
2. **Implement P12 as a uniform scale per island:** an island whose narrowest face is under `min_width` master pixels
   (default 4) is scaled up until that face reaches it, up to a cap (default 8 x the master density). A uniform scale
   keeps the island free of distortion. Store the density per island in the master's `.npz`; the copy and the
   staleness data must use it. Report the master size before and after.
3. **Re-run** `step3_master.py` and `step5_layouts.py` for panel, cap and knob, layouts `abcdefg`, samples 64,
   gt_samples 1024, with a new `suffix` so Session 2's outputs stay.
4. **Inspect the images** of the panel's (d) and (f) differences and say whether the lines are gone.

**Finish line:**

| No. | Criterion |
| :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Step 1's table: share of wrong pixels on faces under 2, 3 and 4 master pixels |
| 2 | Panel seam band MAE at most 1.5 x interior in every layout; no line visible in (d) and (f) |
| 3 | Maximum error per layout no larger than 1.5 x the direct bake's maximum, every object and layout |
| 4 | Session 2's finish lines 2, 3 and 5 still MET (re-measured, not assumed) |
| 5 | Master size and bake time before and after, per object |

A criterion not met is reported as NOT MET with its numbers.

**Do not:** change the artist's UV maps or islands; bake decal or skip materials; touch anything outside
`tests/bake_prototype/`, `tests/_work/` and this journal; push; stage the other uncommitted files in the working tree
(`fss_blender_tools/*.py`, `tests/*.py`, the UV journal, `uv_problems.py`).

## Session 4: P12, Minimum Face Width

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar + Claude (Opus 5.5, sub-agent executing Session 3's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** f9f9db6

Step 1 of the hand-off was run on the panel, and it shows that the narrow-face hypothesis is wrong: most of the copy's
wrong pixels and most of the seam band are on faces 4 master pixels wide or wider. As the hand-off asks, the work
stopped there. P12 (steps 2 to 4) was not implemented, no master was rebuilt and no layout was re-baked. Every Blender
run went through the CPU cap (50 %), with `--factory-startup` and Cycles at 12 threads, one run of ours at a time. A
second `blender.exe` (the maintainer's interactive Blender) ran alongside, so the times measured here may be slower
than they would otherwise be.

### Step 1: Wrong Pixels Against Master Face Width

`p12_measure.py` gives every panel face its master width: the smallest altitude of the triangle (2 x area / longest
edge, in 3D) times the master density (2736.1 px/m, Session 2's master). It also finds the face under every pixel of
layouts (a) and (d) and reads Session 2's copy and GT images (`layouts/panel/`). As a check, it recomputes the copy's
MAE over the inside pixels and gets Session 2's figures (0.0064 and 0.0089).

Of the 632 faces, 300, 439 and 502 are under 2, 3 and 4 master px (the median is 2.21 px, the smallest 0.32 px).
Most of them are slivers of the flat panel, which are harmless, because the copy's read also takes texels of
coplanar neighbours.

| Layout | Pixel set | Pixels | On faces under 2 / 3 / 4 px | Share of the set's summed \|error\| on faces under 2 / 3 / 4 px |
| :--- | :--- | ---: | :--- | :--- |
| a | wrong (\|copy - GT\| > 0.1) | 7 | 14.3 / 14.3 / 28.6 % | 11.7 / 11.7 / 23.0 % |
| a | seam band | 4726 | 5.6 / 15.4 / 18.7 % | 8.4 / 20.6 / 23.6 % |
| a | all inside (reference) | 18643 | 4.8 / 8.9 / 11.8 % | 9.8 / 15.7 / 18.6 % |
| d | wrong (\|copy - GT\| > 0.1) | 101 | 4.0 / 5.0 / 5.0 % | 2.7 / 6.0 / 6.0 % |
| d | seam band | 8980 | 10.2 / 24.8 / 27.8 % | 11.4 / 22.9 / 24.9 % |
| d | all inside (reference) | 67832 | 6.0 / 9.8 / 12.5 % | 10.7 / 15.1 / 17.6 % |

The faces under the wrong pixels of (d): face 288 has 66 of the 101 pixels, 282 has 13, 284 has 8 and 71 has 5, all
of them about 6.9 master px wide. Only 6 wrong pixels lie on faces under 4 px.

What those faces are (`inspect_wrong_faces.py`, layout (d), no bake). Faces 282, 284 and 288 are triangles of the
frame's 2.5 mm strips, 20 to 45 mm long, and each has one edge on the mesh's **open border** (no 3D neighbour). Face 71
is a 2.5 mm by 135 mm strip between two 90 degree folds. All four lie in the master's main island (452 faces). On face
288, the worst pixels lie on the open edge itself (0.00 to 0.01 px from it): the copy is 0.31 to 0.35 there, GT 0.08
to 0.11, and the direct bake 0.06 to 0.20. On face 282, the copy is darker than GT (0.07 to 0.13 against 0.25 to
0.26), half a pixel from an edge. The difference image `layouts/panel/d_diff_copy_x8.png` shows the lines along the
outer rims of both layout islands, the open border and the frame strips next to it; they do not follow the thin faces
inside the panel.

Conclusion: P12 as specified would scale up the master's main island (its narrowest faces are 0.32 px wide; it would
reach the 8 x cap) without touching the pixels that are wrong. The wrong pixels are on the open mesh border and the
strips beside it. Two causes fit what was seen, and neither was measured:

- the master holds no skirt beyond an edge without a neighbour, and Cycles darkens a direct bake right at such an
  edge;
- the 90 degree folds inside one master island (face 71) put another surface's texels next to the strip's.

### Finish Line

| No. | Criterion | Result |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Step 1's table: share of wrong pixels on faces under 2, 3 and 4 master px | MET (the table above). The hypothesis fails: (a) 14.3 / 14.3 / 28.6 % of 7 wrong pixels, (d) 4.0 / 5.0 / 5.0 % of 101; seam band 5.6 / 15.4 / 18.7 % (a) and 10.2 / 24.8 / 27.8 % (d) |
| 2 | Panel seam band MAE at most 1.5 x interior in every layout; no line visible in (d) and (f) | NOT MET, not re-run (stopped after step 1): Session 2's ratios stand, (c) 1.58, (d) 1.69, (e) 2.12, (f) 1.71; lines visible in (d) |
| 3 | Maximum error per layout at most 1.5 x the direct bake's maximum, every object and layout | NOT MET, not re-run: in Session 2's numbers, panel (a) 0.397 against 0.132 (3.0 x), (b) 0.309 / 0.171 (1.8 x), (d) 0.530 / 0.164 (3.2 x), (e) 0.387 / 0.179 (2.2 x). Within 1.5 x: panel (c) 0.148 / 0.108, (f) 0.168 / 0.138, (g) 0.102 / 0.115, and every layout of the cap and the knob |
| 4 | Session 2's finish lines 2, 3 and 5 still MET (re-measured) | Not re-measured for a new master (none was built). The refactored `step5_layouts.py` was re-run on the panel, layouts a, d, f (`suffix=_s4check`), and reproduces Session 2 exactly: MAE copy / direct 0.0064 / 0.0152, 0.0089 / 0.0149, 0.0065 / 0.0149; P99 0.0326 / 0.0537, 0.0394 / 0.0527, 0.0329 / 0.0537; seam / interior 1.49, 1.69, 1.71; 79 faces above master in (d) |
| 5 | Master size and bake time before and after, per object | NOT MET: no "after" master. Before (Session 2): panel 547 x 273 px (core / skirt bake 0.50 / 0.07 s), cap 995 x 887 (4.16 / 0.26 s), knob 843 x 488 (2.18 / 0.14 s) |

Times of this session: `p12_measure.py` and `inspect_wrong_faces.py` take under 2 s each in Blender; the
`_s4check` run of layouts a, d and f took 9.5 s (GT bake 1.21, 4.27 and 1.41 s), with the maintainer's Blender
running alongside.

### Images Inspected

- `tests/_work/ao_bake/layouts/panel/d_diff_copy_x8.png` (Session 2's copy against GT, x 8): red and blue lines along
  the outer rims of both layout islands and along the frame strips by the open border; the button openings show
  only faint rims. Layout (f)'s image was not inspected, because no new (f) was made.

### Proposals (not answered by the maintainer)

- **P14: drop P12 as specified.** It does not reach the wrong pixels, and it would raise the main island to the cap
  because of slivers on the flat panel. If a width rule is still wanted, it would have to measure the width of a
  surface (coplanar neighbours within the read's 30 degrees), not of a triangle.
- **P15: measure the open-border cause next.** For each wrong pixel, its distance to the nearest open edge and to
  the nearest fold of over 30 degrees inside its master island; then try one of two changes: a mirrored skirt beyond
  open edges (the face itself, mirrored), or, more simply, splitting master islands at folds of over 30 degrees,
  so that the surface-aware read never meets another surface inside an island.

`tests/bake_prototype/`: `bake_layouts.py` (the test layouts, moved out of `step5_layouts.py` without change, used
by it and by the step 1 scripts), `p12_measure.py` (step 1), `inspect_wrong_faces.py` (the faces under the wrong
pixels). Outputs: `tests/_work/ao_bake/layouts/panel/p12_measure.json`, `p12_measure.log.txt`,

## Session 5: Hand-off for P15, Errors at Open Borders and Folds

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** a0bcd5e

### Decisions

- **P12 is dropped (P14):** the maintainer asked for P15 next, after Session 4 showed the wrong pixels are not on
  narrow faces.

### Review of Session 4

The logs (`p12_measure.log.txt`, `inspect_wrong_faces.log.txt`) give the numbers Session 4 reports, and
`layouts/panel/d_diff_copy_x8.png` shows the lines along the islands' outer rims and the frame strips. Not re-run.

One more possible cause, not in P15: on face 288 the wrong pixels lie on the open edge itself (distance 0.0 px), and
there the GT is dark (0.08 to 0.11) while the copy is 0.31 to 0.35. Session 3 found that Cycles writes dark values
in its margin at open borders. If those pixels' centres lie outside the face, the GT value there is Cycles' margin,
not AO, and the "error" is the reference's, not the copy's.

### Hand-off: P15

Executed by a Claude sub-agent, which appends Session 6 and commits by name without pushing. The rules for running
anything are those of Session 3's hand-off (PowerShell only; one Blender at a time, through `cpucap.exe 50`, Cycles
at 12 threads or fewer; copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` only; the maintainer's bake rules). A `blender.exe` the
agent did not start is the maintainer's: do not wait for it or stop it.

**Steps:**

1. **Classify the wrong pixels** of the panel in layouts (a), (b), (d) and (e) (copy minus GT above 0.1), and of the
   seam band, into three causes:
   - **GT margin:** the pixel's centre lies outside every face of the layout (the GT there is Cycles' margin);
   - **open border:** within 2 layout pixels of an open mesh edge;
   - **fold:** within 2 layout pixels of an edge whose faces differ by more than 30 degrees and lie in the same master
     island;
   - **other.** A pixel may belong to more than one; report the overlaps.
2. **For the largest cause, try its change, and only that one:**
   - GT margin: measure finish lines 3 and 4 and the maximum error on pixels whose centre lies inside a face, and
     report both the old and the new numbers;
   - open border: a mirrored skirt beyond open edges (the face itself, mirrored);
   - fold: split master islands at folds of more than 30 degrees.
3. **Re-run** panel, cap and knob, layouts `abcdefg`, samples 64, gt_samples 1024, with a new `suffix`, only if
   step 2 changed the master or the copy.
4. **Inspect** the panel's (d) and (f) difference images.

**Finish line:**

| No. | Criterion |
| :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Step 1's table: wrong pixels and seam band per cause, per layout, with overlaps |
| 2 | Panel seam band MAE at most 1.5 x interior in every layout; no line visible in (d) and (f) |
| 3 | Maximum error per layout no larger than 1.5 x the direct bake's maximum, every object and layout |
| 4 | Session 2's finish lines 2, 3 and 5 still MET (re-measured, not assumed) |
| 5 | Master size and bake time before and after, per object, if the master changed |

A criterion not met is reported as NOT MET with its numbers.

**Do not:** change the artist's UV maps or islands; bake decal or skip materials; try more than one change; touch
anything outside `tests/bake_prototype/`, `tests/_work/` and this journal; push; stage the other uncommitted
files in the working tree.

## Session 6: P15, Errors at Open Borders and Folds

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar + Claude (Opus 5.5, sub-agent executing Session 5's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** a0bcd5e

Steps 1 to 4 of Session 5's hand-off were run. Every Blender run went through the CPU cap (50 %), with
`--factory-startup` and Cycles at 12 threads, one run of ours at a time, started from a scratch folder. The
maintainer's interactive `blender.exe` ran alongside the whole time, so the times below may be slower than they would
otherwise be. Only the copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` were used; no UV map of the artist was changed, and the scene
files and their rules (skip materials, tags, animation) are Session 2's, unchanged.

### Step 1: Wrong Pixels and Seam Band by Cause

`p15_classify.py` reads Session 2's images of the panel (`layouts/panel/`) and makes the layouts again. Classes:

- **margin:** Cycles did not count the pixel as inside a face. The prototype's own rasterizer counts every pixel whose
  centre is in a face or on its border, so by that rule no inside pixel has its centre outside every face. To find
  the pixels where Cycles' rule differs, the script bakes each layout once more with margin 0 (AO, 1 sample, about
  0.02 s) and takes the pixels Cycles left unwritten.
- **open:** the pixel centre within 2 layout px of an open mesh edge (24 edges on the panel).
- **fold:** within 2 layout px of an edge whose faces differ by more than 30 degrees (with consistent winding) and lie
  in one master island (98 edges).
- **other:** none of these.

Wrong: inside pixels with \|copy - GT\| above 0.1. Error share: share of the set's summed \|copy - GT\|.

| Layout | Set | Pixels | Margin: px (err) | Open: px (err) | Fold: px (err) | Other: px (err) | Overlaps (px) |
| :--- | :--- | ---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| a | wrong | 7 | 1 (11.3 %) | 5 (77.0 %) | 2 (46.7 %) | 0 | open+fold 1 |
| a | seam band | 4726 | 1 (0.3 %) | 2148 (52.8 %) | 2725 (45.5 %) | 358 (8.5 %) | open+fold 506 |
| b | wrong | 6 | 0 | 3 (40.5 %) | 2 (44.1 %) | 1 (15.5 %) | none |
| b | seam band | 5764 | 0 | 2135 (43.2 %) | 2761 (41.7 %) | 1342 (19.9 %) | open+fold 474 |
| d | wrong | 101 | 3 (3.4 %) | 95 (93.0 %) | 3 (5.1 %) | 4 (3.0 %) | margin+open 3, open+fold 1 |
| d | seam band | 8980 | 4 (0.5 %) | 4031 (57.0 %) | 4185 (34.6 %) | 796 (9.0 %) | margin+open 4, open+fold 32 |
| e | wrong | 126 | 0 | 110 (88.1 %) | 4 (2.8 %) | 14 (10.2 %) | open+fold 2 |
| e | seam band | 11790 | 0 | 5277 (64.1 %) | 5471 (28.7 %) | 1074 (9.1 %) | open+fold 32 |

Pixels Cycles does not count as inside: 1, 0, 0, 4, 0, 4 and 0 of the inside pixels in layouts (a) to (g). The
"GT margin" cause of Session 5's review is therefore negligible: at most 3 of 101 wrong pixels in (d). The open
border is the largest cause: it holds most of the wrong pixels in (a), (d) and (e) and half in (b), and the largest
share of the seam band's error in every layout. Folds hold more seam band pixels than the open border, but a smaller
share of the error (except in (b), 41.7 against 43.2 %).

### Step 2: The Change Tried, a Mirrored Skirt Beyond Open Edges

Only this change was made. `bake_unfold.build_master(..., mirror_open=True)`: a border edge of an island without a 3D
neighbour gets the face itself as its skirt piece, hinged across the edge (its own third corner mirrored to the far
side) and clipped to the skirt band as before. The skirt piece keeps the face's 3D positions, so the bake reads the
face's own surface near the edge, mirrored, where Session 2 had no skirt (the corner fill took the nearest pixel of
the rectangle). Its face map entry is the face itself. `step3_master.py` takes `mirror_open=1` and `suffix=`;
`step5_layouts.py` takes `master_suffix=`. The masters were rebuilt as `master_<tag>_p15` (Session 2's stay).

| Bake core / skirt after, s | 0.44 / 0.09 | 3.45 / 0.26 | 1.88 / 0.15 |

### Images Inspected

- `tests/_work/ao_bake/layouts/panel_p15/d_diff_copy_x8.png` against Session 2's `layouts/panel/d_diff_copy_x8.png`:
  hardly any difference to the eye. The red line along the top and bottom outer rims of the first island and the
  blue and red rims of the second island are still there; the button openings show only faint rims.
- `tests/_work/ao_bake/layouts/panel_p15/f_diff_copy_x8.png`: the thin Smart UV strips are mostly red or blue over
  their whole width, and the larger islands have lines along their rims. These images also show the padding (copy
  against Cycles' margin, which differs by 0.1 to 0.3 by design, Session 3 finding 3), and on the thin strips the
  padding is most of what is drawn, so the image overstates the inside error; (f)'s inside error is still the seam
  ratio 1.68 above.

### Proposals (not answered by the maintainer)

- **P16: keep the mirrored skirt.** It is cheap (master size unchanged) and lowers the panel's wrong pixels (d)
  101 to 77, (e) 126 to 64 and the maximum in (a) and (e), but it is not enough on its own.
- **P17: measure what the GT holds at the open border before another change.** On face 288 (Session 4) the GT is
  0.08 to 0.11 right at the open edge, and the copy 0.31 to 0.35. Whatever the master holds beyond the edge, a
  bilinear read at the edge gives the value about half a master pixel inside the face; if AO falls steeply within that
  half pixel (the strip's open edge touching another object), only a denser master along open edges would follow it.
  One measurement: the direct GT along a line across the edge at a few times the master density.
- **P18: the fold cause next,** if the maintainer wants the seam band ratio: splitting master islands at folds over
  30 degrees (Session 5's third option), as the folds hold about half the seam band's pixels.

`mirror_open` option in `bake_unfold.py`, `step3_master.py` (with `suffix`) and `step5_layouts.py` (with
`master_suffix`). Outputs: `tests/_work/ao_bake/master/master_<tag>_p15.*`, `layouts/<tag>_p15/`,
`layouts/panel/p15_classify.*` and `layouts/panel_p15/p15_classify.*`.

looks like Session 2's, with the rim lines still there. The knob's master PNG is byte-identical to Session 2's
although 20 edges were mirrored; probably those edges are at the knob's hidden base, which bakes black either way
(not checked).

### Decisions

  answered.

### Hand-off: P18

Executed by a Claude sub-agent, which appends Session 8 and commits by name without pushing. Rules for running
anything as in Session 3's hand-off: PowerShell only; one Blender at a time, through `cpucap.exe 50`, Cycles at 12
threads or fewer; copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` only; the maintainer's bake rules (existing UV maps, no decal or
skip materials, tags read, movable objects checked). A `blender.exe` the agent did not start is the maintainer's: do
not wait for it or stop it.

**Steps:**

1. **Split master islands at folds:** an edge whose two faces' normals differ by more than the copy's surface angle
   (30 degrees) is never joined inside a master island. Keep Session 6's `mirror_open` on, so the result compares with
   Session 6.
2. **The skirt at a split edge:** the copy's read skips texels of another surface, so a neighbour face unfolded
   across a fold gives it nothing. Use the face mirrored across the edge there (as `mirror_open` does), and report
   the island count and the master size it costs.
3. **Re-run** panel, cap and knob, layouts `abcdefg`, samples 64, gt_samples 1024, with a new `suffix`, and repeat
   Session 6's step 1 classification on the panel so the fold share before and after can be compared.
4. **Inspect** the panel's (d) and (f) difference images, inside pixels apart from the padding.

**Finish line:**

| No. | Criterion |

| 2 | Fold share of the panel's seam band error, before and after, per layout |
| 3 | Maximum error per layout no larger than 1.5 x the direct bake's maximum, every object and layout (expected NOT MET at the open edges; report it) |
| 4 | Session 2's finish lines 2, 3 and 5 still MET (re-measured); the knob's side still one island |
| 5 | Island count, master size and bake time before and after, per object |

A criterion not met is reported as NOT MET with its numbers.

## Session 8: P18, Master Islands Split at Folds

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar + Claude (Opus 5.5, sub-agent executing Session 7's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** 58c7f74

Steps 1 to 4 of Session 7's hand-off were run. Every Blender run went through the CPU cap (50 %), with
`--factory-startup` and Cycles at 12 threads, one run of ours at a time, started from a scratch folder. The
maintainer's interactive `blender.exe` ran during the master builds and the panel's and part of the cap's layout runs,
so those times may be slower than they would otherwise be. Only the copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` were used; no UV
map of the artist was changed, and the scene files and their rules (skip materials, tags, animated objects) are
Session 2's, unchanged.

### Steps 1 and 2: What Changed

`bake_unfold.build_master(..., fold_split=30)` (`step3_master.py -- fold_split=30`), with Session 6's `mirror_open` on:

- **Islands:** the islands grow as before (`growlen`, the longest shared edge first), but never across an edge whose
  two faces' normals differ by more than 30 degrees (the existing fold limit of `Islands._grow`, which `growlen30`
  sets). An edge over the limit is also never counted as an inner edge, even where a closing fan would make it
  continuous; no such edge occurred (0 on all three objects).
- **Skirt at a split edge:** a border edge whose nearest 3D neighbour (the smallest fold) lies across more than 30
  degrees gets the face itself, mirrored across the edge, as its skirt piece, as `mirror_open` does at open edges. Its
  face map entry is the face itself.

The masters were rebuilt as `master_<tag>_p18`; Session 6's `master_<tag>_p15` are the "before".

| | panel | cap | knob |
| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Islands before / after | 23 / 31 | 14 / 6 | 1 / 1 |
| Joins refused (overlap) before / after | 216 / 113 | 117 / 0 | 0 / 0 |
| Border edges mirrored: open / at a split fold | 24 / 296 | 40 / 400 | 20 / 0 |
| Skirt pieces before / after | 1023 / 1029 | 932 / 892 | 440 / 440 |
| Skirt pixels before / after | 9819 / 15547 | 18337 / 52501 | 9997 / 9997 |
| Master size before, px | 547 x 273 (149331) | 995 x 887 (882565) | 843 x 488 (411384) |
| Master size after, px | 498 x 452 (225096, +51 %) | 2127 x 724 (1539948, +74 %) | same (PNG byte-identical) |
| Bake core / skirt before, s | 0.44 / 0.09 | 3.45 / 0.26 | 1.88 / 0.15 |
| Bake core / skirt after, s | 0.41 / 0.13 | 3.50 / 0.54 | 1.85 / 0.20 |
| PNG before / after, kB | 60.5 / 64.3 | 365.2 / 384.2 | 125.4 / 125.4 |

The cap needs fewer islands, not more: with the fold limit the growth no longer runs into overlaps (117 refused joins
to 0), and each of its three walls around Z is now one island (Session 2: one of them in 11 islands). Its master is
wide (one long island sets the shelf width). The knob has no fold over 30 degrees between faces, so its master did not
change.

## Session 9: Review of Session 8

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** b76adad

### What Was Re-Checked

- **Commit b76adad** holds only the prototype scripts, this journal and the index row.
- **Result files (not re-run):** the panel's seam and interior MAE, ratios and maxima in `layouts/panel_p15/` and
  `layouts/panel_p18/results.json` agree with Session 8's tables.
- **Images:** `layouts/panel_p18/d_inside_diff_copy_x8.png` (inside pixels only) shows only a faint rim along the
  islands' outer edges. The strong lines of the earlier difference images were mostly padding, where the copy differs
  from Cycles' margin by design (Session 3, finding 3), so the "line visible" part of the seam criterion was judged
  on the wrong images in Sessions 2, 4 and 6.

### Findings

1. **Against a normal direct bake, the copy's seams are already better.** Panel seam band MAE copy / direct (64
   samples) after Session 8: a 0.0072 / 0.0172, b 0.0071 / 0.0169, c 0.0064 / 0.0173, d 0.0127 / 0.0183,
   e 0.0163 / 0.0179, f 0.0077 / 0.0164, g 0.0051 / 0.0172. P99 is lower for the copy in 6 of 7; in (e) it is 0.111
   against 0.070. The 1.5 x criterion compares the copy's seams with its own interior, which is much better than a
   direct bake's, so it is stricter than "as good as a normal bake".
2. **The fold split did not act on the fold edges:** their share of the seam band error stayed the same (for example
   d 36.5 to 35.0 %). The ratio fell mostly where the seam MAE fell at open borders and, in (a), (c) and (f), partly
   because the interior MAE rose (Session 8).
3. **What remains is a few pixels at open edges** of the panel's 2.5 mm frame strips: maxima 0.293 (b), 0.166 (c),
   0.489 (d) against 0.171, 0.108, 0.164 for the direct bake. Cap and knob meet every line.
## Session 10: Hand-off for a 4 x Master and One Tuning Pass

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** b76adad

### Decisions

- **Test a master at 4 x the layout's density** (today 2 x, `D = 2.0 * median_d` in `step3_master.py`), and then
  one more tuning pass if a change is left that can still lower the error (maintainer, 20261112).

Why 4 x: the median ratio of master pixels per layout pixel is 2.0 in the panel's layouts (a), (b), 1.05 in (d) and
0.80 in (e); the panel's worst pixels and most wrong pixels are in (d) and (e). At 4 x, (d) and (e) would read
about 2.1 and 1.6 master pixels per layout pixel. Layout (c) (ratio 4) still has a maximum of 1.53 x the direct
bake's, so 4 x alone is not expected to clear the open edges.

### Hand-off

Executed by a Claude sub-agent, which appends Session 11 and commits by name without pushing. Rules for running
anything as in Session 3's hand-off: PowerShell only; one Blender at a time, through `cpucap.exe 50`, Cycles at 12
threads or fewer; copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` only; the maintainer's bake rules (existing UV maps, no decal or
skip materials, tags read, movable objects checked). A `blender.exe` the agent did not start is the maintainer's: do
not wait for it or stop it.

**Steps:**

1. **4 x master:** add a `density=` argument to `step3_master.py` (factor over the layout's median, default 2).
   Build panel, cap and knob at 4 x with Session 8's settings (`mirror_open` and `fold_split=30` on), re-run layouts
   `abcdefg`, samples 64, gt_samples 1024, new `suffix`. Compare with Session 8 per object and layout.
2. **Measure what is left (P17):** on the panel's worst faces at the open edges after step 1, the GT along short
   lines across the edge at 4 or more times the master density, against the copy along the same lines. Report
   whether the remaining error is a drop narrower than a master pixel, or something else.
3. **One tuning pass, only if step 2 points to a change that can still lower the error.** Pick one, say why from
   step 2's numbers, and measure it against step 1:
   - **denser band along open edges:** faces touching an open edge get their own master islands at a higher density
     (the narrow-face idea of P12, applied only where step 2 shows the error);
   - **more master samples:** if the remaining error is noise rather than a drop, bake the master with more samples
     (it is small, so this is cheap);
   - **another change** that step 2's numbers point to, explained.
   If step 2 shows nothing a change can reach, skip step 3 and say so.
4. **Inspect** the panel's (d) and (e) inside-only difference images (Session 8's `inside_diff` approach) before and
   after.

**Finish line:**

| No. | Criterion |
| :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Per object and layout, 2 x (Session 8) against 4 x: seam / interior, maximum and its ratio to the direct bake, number of wrong pixels (above 0.1) |
| 2 | Panel seam band MAE at most 1.5 x interior in every layout, at 4 x and after step 3 |
| 3 | Maximum error per layout no larger than 1.5 x the direct bake's, every object and layout, at 4 x and after step 3 |
| 4 | Step 2's profile across the open edges, with its conclusion |
| 5 | Session 2's finish lines 2, 3 and 5 still MET (re-measured) |
| 6 | Master size (pixels and kB) and bake time, 2 x against 4 x and after step 3, per object |

A criterion not met is reported as NOT MET with its numbers.

**Do not:** change the artist's UV maps or islands; bake decal or skip materials; try more than one change in step 3;
touch anything outside `tests/bake_prototype/`, `tests/_work/` and this journal; push; stage the other uncommitted
files in the working tree.

## Session 11: 4 x Master and Tuning Pass

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar + Claude (Opus 5.5, sub-agent executing Session 10's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** b76adad (Sessions 9 and 10 were not committed yet and are committed with this session)

Steps 1 to 4 of Session 10's hand-off were run. Every Blender run went through the CPU cap (50 %), with
`--factory-startup` and Cycles at 12 threads, started from a scratch folder. The maintainer's interactive `blender.exe`
ran from the 4 x cap layouts until the profile, so the cap's and knob's layout times and the profile's bake time may be
slower than they would otherwise be. One slip: the summary script (`s11_summary.py`, reads PNGs, no bake, a few
seconds) was started once while the same-face knob run was still going, so two of ours ran together for those
seconds; its partial output was discarded and it was run again afterwards. Only the copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/`
were used; no UV map of the artist was changed, and the scene files and their rules (skip materials, tags, animated
objects) are Session 2's, unchanged.

### Step 1: The 4 x Master

`step3_master.py` takes `density=` (factor over layout (a)'s median, default 2). The masters were built with Session
8's settings: `-- tag=<tag> samples=64 mirror_open=1 fold_split=30 density=4 suffix=_s11x4`. The islands are the
same as Session 8's (they do not depend on the density); only the pixel size changes, with the gutter still 2 px.

| npz 2 x / 4 x, kB | 30.5 / 51.8 | 54.1 / 123.5 | 25.3 / 57.7 |

`step5_layouts.py -- tag=<tag> samples=64 gt_samples=1024 layouts=abcdefg suffix=_s11x4 master_suffix=_s11x4`:
panel 28.9 s, cap 202.0 s, knob 99.0 s (Session 8: 21.8, 161.5, 86.1). The copy takes longer because each layout pixel
covers twice as many master pixels per axis (cap (b): 3.6 to 18.3 s); the GT bakes are unchanged.

### Step 2: Profile Across the Open Edges (P17)

`s11_profile.py -- tag=panel suffix=_s11x4 masters=_s11x4,_p18 layouts=bde faces=6 per_face=2 factor=4
gt_samples=4096`. After step 1 the panel's wrong pixels (inside, \|copy - GT\| > 0.1) are 2 in (b), 10 in (d) and 7 in
(e); 0, 9 and 6 of them lie within 2 layout px of an open edge, on five faces: 284 (summed error 1.36, 4 px), 475
(0.55, 5 px), 24 (0.33, 3 px), 71 (0.23, 2 px) and 476 (0.11, 1 px). (b)'s two lie elsewhere and were not profiled.

The script bakes those faces, and face 70 whose open edge is nearest to face 71's pixels, each on its own in its
master UV at 4 x the 4 x master's density (21888 px/m, a quarter of a master pixel; 4096 samples, margin 0, 104 s):
GT_hi. For the two worst pixels per face and layout it follows a line from the open edge into the face, perpendicular
to the edge, and reads GT_hi (averaged over 7 hi-res px along the edge) and each master with the copy's read. It also
re-samples the layout pixel itself from GT_hi and from each master (8 x 8 points on its own triangle) and lists the
master texels the read uses. Distances in pixels of the 4 x master:

| Layout, face (pixel) | Copy / GT / direct of the pixel | Pixel from GT_hi / 4 x / 2 x master | GT_hi at 0, 0.5, 1, 2, 4 px | 4 x master at 0, 0.5, 1, 2, 4 px | What the 4 x read takes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| d, 284 (778,122), on the edge | 0.100 / 0.493 / 0.500 | 0.077 / 0.119 / 0.179 | 0.026, 0.026, 0.232, 0.491, 0.502 | 0.034, 0.089, 0.168, 0.500, 0.508 | own face only |
| d, 284 (778,124), on the edge | 0.119 / 0.492 / 0.504 | 0.077 / 0.141 / 0.195 | 0.026, 0.026, 0.230, 0.491, 0.502 | 0.044, 0.116, 0.206, 0.512, 0.500 | own face only |
| d, 475 (456,173), 0.39 px | 0.371 / 0.485 / 0.500 | 0.487 / 0.372 / 0.398 | -, 0.485 (face 0.5 px wide) | 0.445, 0.335 | 50 % face 476 (texels 0.264) |
| d, 475 (455,173), 0.39 px | 0.381 / 0.490 / 0.484 | 0.487 / 0.380 / 0.419 | -, 0.485 | 0.464, 0.340 | 54 % face 476 (0.274) |
| e, 475 (591,224), 0.39 px | 0.361 / 0.487 / 0.500 | 0.485 / 0.361 / 0.410 | -, 0.485 | 0.461, 0.343 | 54 % face 476 (0.249) |
| e, 475 (586,224), 0.39 px | 0.384 / 0.485 / 0.516 | 0.485 / 0.405 / 0.413 | -, 0.484 | 0.457, 0.364 | 42 % face 476 (0.301) |
| e, 24 (560,209), 0.58 px | 0.327 / 0.456 / 0.453 | 0.454 / 0.356 / 0.359 | -, 0.452 | 0.432, 0.326 | 47 % face 23 (0.236) |
| e, 24 (560,210), 0.58 px | 0.354 / 0.456 / 0.438 | 0.456 / 0.361 / 0.381 | -, 0.450 | 0.426, 0.319 | 43 % face 23 (0.237) |
| d, 71 (747,12), 1.19 px from face 70's edge | 0.267 / 0.145 / 0.078 | 0.131 / 0.231 / 0.192 | on face 70: 0.475, 0.462, 0.448 | on face 70: 0.457, 0.463, 0.469 | 27 % face 70 (0.468) |
| d, 71 (748,12) | 0.263 / 0.156 / 0.125 | 0.135 / 0.228 / 0.179 | on face 70: 0.473, 0.460 | on face 70: 0.446, 0.450 | 27 % face 70 (0.456) |
| e, 476 (573,224), 0.39 px from 475's edge | 0.288 / 0.394 / 0.344 | 0.375 / 0.310 / 0.336 | (line leaves the face) | | 21 % face 475 (0.483) |

Every neighbour named in the last column is coplanar with the face read (0.0 degrees), so the copy's 30 degree
surface rule lets the read take it. The re-sampled pixel from the 4 x master agrees with the copy (within 0.04), so the
profile explains the copy's values.

Conclusion:

- **Face 284 (the (d) maximum, 0.50):** the GT does drop at the open edge: 0.03 up to 0.5 px from the edge, 0.23 at
  1 px, 0.49 at 2 px, so the drop is about 1 px of the 4 x master wide (half a pixel of the 2 x master). The 4 x
  master follows it (\|master - GT_hi\| 0.056 to 0.081 below 1 px, 0.12 to 0.13 for the 2 x master). But the layout
  pixel's error is not the master's: the same pixel from GT_hi is 0.077, the copy 0.100 and 0.119, while Cycles' bake at
  the layout's resolution gives 0.49 to 0.50 in both the GT (1024 samples) and the direct bake (64). At pixels whose
  centre lies on the open edge, Cycles' layout bake reads a value that its own high-resolution bake of the same
  points does not have. Why was not measured (Cycles' bake source not read). No master change reaches this error.
- **Faces 475, 24, 71, 476:** no drop at the open edge (GT_hi flat on the face, 0.485 to 0.489 on 475); the error is
  a step in AO across an inner edge to a coplanar neighbour (475 about 0.49 against 476 about 0.26 to 0.38; 24 0.46
  against 23 0.24; 71 0.13 against 70 0.47). The bilinear read takes 21 to 54 % of its weight from the neighbour's
  texels. 475 and 24 are about half a 4 x master pixel wide, so they have almost no texels of their own (0 to 9 % of
  the weight); a denser master raised the neighbour's share (475: 21 to 29 % at 2 x, 42 to 54 % at 4 x), because the
  2 x master's texels there were mostly the face's own mirrored skirt.
- **Noise:** none of the profiled errors is noise; they are 0.1 to 0.4 and systematic, and the copy's inside MAE is
  already 0.26 to 0.44 x the 64-sample direct bake's.

So the remaining error is a drop narrower than a master pixel only in the sense of an AO step at a face edge inside
the panel's flat surface, and a disagreement of the reference at open-edge pixels; neither is a drop across the open
edge that the master misses.

### Step 3: One Change, the Copy Reads the Face's Own Texels First

Chosen from step 2: the error the master can reach is the read taking a coplanar neighbour's texels across an AO step
(faces 475, 24, 71, 476), and those faces do have texels showing them (their own mirrored skirt or fill, 41 to 58 % of
the weight on 475 and 24, 73 % on 71). Not chosen: a denser band along open edges (face 284's error is the reference's,
and for the slivers more density raised the neighbour's share), more master samples (the errors are not noise).

`bake_copy.copy_master(..., same_face=True)` (`step5_layouts.py -- same_face=1`): when the four bilinear texels of a
read include texels whose face map is the sampled face, only those are used (weights renormalised); otherwise the
read is unchanged (texels within 30 degrees). The master is unchanged (4 x). Run: `step5_layouts.py -- tag=<tag>
samples=64 gt_samples=1024 layouts=abcdefg suffix=_s11sf master_suffix=_s11x4 same_face=1` (panel 29.3 s, cap 202.3
s, knob 99.8 s).

  open edge out of the maximum line: at face 284 the layout GT (0.49) and the direct bake (0.50) agree with each other

- **P25: AO steps between coplanar faces** (an occluder resting on the panel along a face edge, probably) are blurred
  by any read that interpolates across faces; only a master whose islands are split where the AO jumps would keep
  them sharp. Not measured; only worth it if such steps are visible in textures.

Executed by a Claude sub-agent, which appends Session 14 and commits by name without pushing. Rules for running

threads or fewer; copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` only; the maintainer's bake rules (existing UV maps, no decal or
skip materials, tags read, movable objects checked). A `blender.exe` the agent did not start is the maintainer's: do
not wait for it or stop it.

**Steps:**

1. **Fair reference (P23):** for each layout, bake Cycles at 4 x the layout's resolution (same 1024 samples per
   pixel, or the most the time allows, stated), then reduce it to the layout's resolution with the copy's own rule:
   each layout pixel averages only the high-resolution samples that lie on the pixel's own triangle (the triangle
   the copy assigns to that pixel). Document the rule in the script.
2. **Re-measure** the 4 x copy (Session 11's `panel_s11x4`, `cap_s11x4`, `knob_s11x4` outputs, or a re-run without the
   same-face read) and the 64-sample direct bake against both references: the old GT and the fair one. Layouts

## Session 14: Fair Reference and Panel (b)'s Maximum

- **Date:** 20261112
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar + Claude (Opus 5.5, sub-agent executing Session 13's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** 1702588 (Sessions 12 and 13 were not committed yet and are committed with this session)

Steps 1 to 4 of Session 13's hand-off were run. Every Blender run went through the CPU cap (50 %), with
`--factory-startup` and Cycles at 12 threads, one run of ours at a time, started from a scratch folder. The
maintainer's interactive `blender.exe` ran during all of it, so the bake times may be slower than they would
otherwise be. Only the copies in `tests/_work/ao_bake/` were used; no UV map of the artist was changed, and the scene
files and their rules (skip materials, tags, animated objects) are Session 2's, unchanged. The master and the copy
were not changed: the copy, GT and direct images are Session 11's `layouts/<tag>_s11x4/` (4 x master, no same-face
read).

### Step 1: The Fair Reference (FAIR)

`s14_fair_ref.py -- tag=<tag> suffix=_s11x4 samples=<n> factor=4 layouts=abcdefg`. The rule, as written in the
script:

1. Each layout is baked by Cycles directly (AO, as the GT) at 4 x the layout's resolution: every layout pixel is
   4 x 4 hi-res pixels. Margin 0, seed 301. Large crops are baked in tiles of 512 x 512 layout pixels (the UV map
   shifted and scaled per tile; tiles without a triangle skipped). The layouts are rebuilt by `bake_layouts.py` and
   checked against Session 11's crop.
2. Each layout pixel whose centre lies on a triangle (the copy's owner, `rasterize_ids` at k = 1) takes the mean of the
   hi-res pixels inside it whose centre lies on that same triangle (`rasterize_ids` at k = 4, whose points are the
   hi-res pixel centres) and that Cycles baked. This is the copy's own rule (only points on the pixel's own triangle
   count), with Cycles' hi-res pixels as the points.
3. A pixel whose triangle covers no hi-res centre in it has no FAIR value; it is left out of every comparison and
   counted (1 to 93 per layout).

**Samples:** at 1024 per hi-res pixel all three objects would take about 16 x Session 11's GT time (226 s), about
an hour. The panel was baked at 1024 (250 s), the cap and the knob at 256 per hi-res pixel (502 s and 305 s), 17.6
minutes in all. At 256, a layout pixel fully on one triangle still averages 16 x 256 = 4096 samples; a pixel with one
hi-res sample has 256.

Agreement with the old GT inside faces, away from their edges (interior pixels whose 16 hi-res centres all lie on the
pixel's own triangle), and over all inside pixels:

| Object | Samples per hi-res px | Undefined px (of inside) | Full interior px | GT - FAIR there: MAE, bias | GT - FAIR, all inside: MAE, max |
| :--- | ---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| panel | 1024 | 13 to 93 (g: 93 of 1206) | 200 to 89856 | 0.0020 to 0.0023, -0.0001 to +0.0001 | 0.0022 to 0.0048, 0.063 to 0.485 |
| cap | 256 | 1 to 21 | 4191 to 738993 | 0.0027 to 0.0037, -0.0001 to +0.0000 | 0.0026 to 0.0035, 0.021 to 0.135 |
| knob | 256 | 1 to 5 | 2171 to 378581 | 0.0021 to 0.0024, -0.0001 to +0.0000 | 0.0021 to 0.0027, 0.023 to 0.074 |

Inside the faces the two references are the same AO: no bias, and a difference (0.002 to 0.004) smaller than the
copy's own MAE against either. The large single differences (up to 0.485) are edge pixels, step 3.
