## Session 26: JPEG XL Masters, Hand-off

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** bee8560

### Measured (not by the tool: ffmpeg 7.0.2's libjxl, lossless, effort 7)

| Master | PNG (0.55.2) | JPEG XL lossless | Smaller by |
| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Floor, 214 Mpx, 64 samples | 23 298 227 B | 13 232 398 B (4.7 s) | 43 % |
| Cap | 1 428 622 B | 876 281 B | 39 % |
| Panel | 210 766 B | 127 923 B | 39 % |

Decoded back to 16-bit grey, every file was identical to its PNG's pixels. PNG with Paeth filters and zlib level 9
gained nothing (floor 23 380 326 B). Blender 5.2 neither reads nor writes JPEG XL (its image formats: AVIF, JPEG,
OpenEXR, PNG, WebP, BMP, Cineon, DPX, Iris, JPEG 2000, HDR, Targa, TIFF), and its Python has no JPEG XL package.

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 37: masters are stored as lossless JPEG XL, encoded and decoded by the reference library (libjxl)
  shipped in the add-on** (maintainer, 20261113; amends decisions 18 and 21). The encoder is in this repository, so no
  PC needs other software installed. Python reaches the DLLs through `ctypes`, not through a Python package (jxlpy,
  imagecodecs and pillow-jxl take whole images, which decision 33 rules out, and are tied to one Python version).

**Order:** this hand-off runs before Session 25's. Its agent appends Session 27 and releases 0.56.0; Session 25's
hand-off then runs on top of it, as 0.57.0, its agent appending the next free session number.

### Hand-off

Executed by an agent, which appends Session 27 and commits by name without pushing. The rules for running anything
and the do-not list of Session 16 apply, with Session 19's safety rule (every Blender of the agent's is watched and
ended before reporting) and Session 22's (PowerShell only, never Git Bash). Production files are read only.

1. **The library:**
   - The Windows x64 DLLs of a released libjxl version (the jxl library, its threads library and what they need,
     such as brotli and highway), taken only from libjxl's official GitHub releases (`github.com/libjxl/libjxl`), or
     built from that release's source if it ships no DLLs. State the version, each file's SHA-256 and its size.
   - A build from source is a C++ build and follows the machine's CPU rule: only through
     `C:\Users\uray\.claude\tools\cpucap\cpucap.exe 50 <command>`, at most 12 threads (`-j12`, `/MP12`,
     `CL_MPCount=12`, `-m:1`), never a plain `cmake --build`, `msbuild`, `ninja` or `make -j`, never `/affinity`, and
     never at the same time as a Blender or another build.
   - In `fss_blender_tools/lib/jxl/windows-x64/`, with the licence texts of every library shipped (libjxl BSD-3,
     brotli, highway), and binary in `.gitattributes`. `tools/deploy.ps1` already copies every file of an add-on;
     check it with `-DryRun` (do not deploy).
2. **A `ctypes` wrapper** (one module) that keeps decision 33's paging:
   - **Encode** a 16-bit grey master lossless, from rows produced top to bottom, with memory independent of the
     master's size: libjxl's chunked frame input (`JxlEncoderAddChunkedFrame`) and an output processor writing the
     file as it goes. If the encoder asks for regions out of the order the bake produces them, bridge it (for example
     a raw uint16 scratch file in the `.bake` folder, deleted afterwards) and state the disk it takes at 856 Mpx.
   - **Decode** row by row into Apply's existing tiled scratch files (`JxlDecoderSetImageOutCallback` or
     equivalent), never the whole image in memory.
   - Threads: libjxl's parallel runner with at most the bake's thread setting (12 by default).
   - Effort: measure efforts 1, 3, 5 and 7 on the 214 Mpx floor (size, time, peak), pick one and say why.
3. **Storage:** `<stem>.ao.jxl` replaces `<stem>.ao.png` (schema 3); a re-bake removes the old PNG. PNG masters of
   0.55 (schemas 1 and 2) are still read and applied. If the library cannot be loaded (another OS, a missing file),
   the bake writes PNG as before and the report says so once.
4. **Everything else unchanged:** the map, the layout, the copy, the preview, the panel.
5. **Version 0.56.0;** `fss_blender_tools/README.md` describes the library and its licence; the artist guide waits.

**Finish line:**

What they need, read from their import tables: `jxl.dll` imports `brotlienc`, `brotlidec` and `jxl_cms`; the brotli
DLLs import `brotlicommon`; all import only the Microsoft C++ runtime (`MSVCP140.dll`, `VCRUNTIME140.dll`, the
`api-ms-win-crt` set) besides the system. Blender ships that runtime (`blender.crt/`) and has it loaded. Highway is
linked into `jxl.dll` (no `hwy.dll` in the release); skcms is inside `jxl_cms.dll`, so its licence is shipped too. The
zip's other files (tools, `jpeg62`, `libpng16`, `libwebp`, `gif`, `zlib1`, headers, import libraries) are not
needed. `.gitattributes` (new) marks `*.dll` binary.

### What Changed

| File | What |
| :--- | :--- |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_jxl.py` (new) | The `ctypes` wrapper: loads the six DLLs by their full paths (dependencies first); `available()`, `error()`, `version()`, `reset()`; `FSS_AO_BAKE_NO_JXL` makes the load fail (tests). `Writer` (rows from the top, as `PngRowWriter` takes them), `encode_raw`, `decode_rows` (a sink gets pieces of rows), `read_whole`, `info`, `JxlError` (an `OSError`) |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_core.py` | `SCHEMA` 3; `jxl()` imports the wrapper from the package or by its path (the second Blender loads core by its path); `image_paths`, `master_paths` (the image is the `.ao.jxl`, or the `.ao.png` when only that exists), `all_master_files` (a rename moves all four), `read_image`; `MasterWriter` writes JPEG XL when the library loads, else PNG (`format`, `fallback`, `stats`), and its `finish()` removes the image of the other format; `load_master` and `open_master` read both; `_TiledSink` fills Apply's tiled scratch file from the decoder's pieces in any order |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_runner.py` | One log line per run with the format (`masters as lossless JPEG XL (libjxl 0.12.0, effort 7)`, or PNG and why); `status.json` gets `master_format` (and `jxl_error`); the index entry gets `image` (format, bytes, encoding seconds, scratch bytes, regions, effort, threads, library) |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake.py` | The bake report adds one line when the masters were written as PNG |

| `fss_blender_tools/__init__.py`, `README.md`, `tests/README.md` | Version 0.57.0 (below); the module row and a section on the library and its licences; the suite row |

### How It Works (Hand-off Step 2)

- **Encode.** libjxl's chunked frame input pulls rectangles of at most 2048 x 2048 px, in its own order, from inside
  `JxlEncoderAddChunkedFrame`; the bake pushes rows from the top as bands are composed. The bridge is the one the
  hand-off names: the rows go to a raw uint16 scratch file, `<stem>.ao.jxl.tmp.raw` in the `.bake` folder (2 bytes per
  master pixel: 428 751 932 B at 214 Mpx, **1 712 531 976 B (1.59 GiB) at 856 Mpx**, measured), and after the last
  band the encoder reads each rectangle from it (one read per row of the rectangle, the buffer kept until the encoder
  releases it). An output processor writes the codestream to `<stem>.ao.jxl.tmp` as it comes, with seek-based
  streaming output (`OUTPUT_MODE` 1: group data first, then the header and TOC written back) and streamed input
  (`BUFFERING` 2). The scratch file is deleted after the encode or on Cancel. The encoder asked for 56 rectangles at
  214 Mpx and 210 at 856 Mpx.
- **Decode.** The file is handed to `JxlDecoder` 4 MB at a time; the pixels arrive through
  `JxlDecoderSetImageOutCallback` as pieces of rows, group by group, and `_TiledSink` places them into Apply's tiled
  scratch file (`<stem>.ao.page`, tiles of 256 px, as for a PNG): a row of tiles is buffered until all its pixels came,
  then written and dropped. With one decoder thread at most 2 rows of tiles were open (856 Mpx: 2 x 256 x 30 464 x 2 B
  = 31 MB); with 12 threads 26 (0.43 GB peak) and not faster, because the Python callback is the limit; Apply decodes
  with one thread (`DECODE_THREADS`), the bake encodes with the bake's thread setting (at most 12).
- **Lossless, 16-bit grey:** one colour channel, 16 bits, `uses_original_profile`, linear grey colour encoding,
  `JxlEncoderSetFrameLossless`.

### Effort (Hand-off Step 2)

The 214 Mpx floor's master of Session 24 (`s24/floor/floor_4096.bake`, PNG 23 298 227 B) encoded through `Writer`,
12 threads, under the cap (`s27/effort/probe_runs.jsonl`):

| Effort | Bytes | Against the PNG | Encode | Peak working set |
| ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1 | 58 336 406 | 2.50 x larger | 3.24 s | 0.066 GB |
| 3 | 62 393 384 | 2.68 x larger | 2.18 s | 0.162 GB |
| 5 | 13 944 394 | 40.2 % smaller | 3.11 s | 0.162 GB |
| 7 | 13 193 871 | 43.4 % smaller | 3.90 s | 0.164 GB |

Effort 7 is the default: the smallest file, 0.8 s more than effort 5, and the encoder's memory (under 0.2 GB) is not
what sets the bake's peak. Efforts 1 and 3 do not suit the masters' smooth 16-bit values. Decode of the effort-7 file:
6.6 s with 12 threads, 9.7 s with one, peak 0.06 GB; decoded back, identical to the PNG's pixels.

### Not Verified

- The visible run and its preview (unchanged code; no window was opened).
- Another OS: only the fallback through the switch; no macOS or Linux library is shipped (the bake writes PNG there,
  and a `.jxl` master made on Windows cannot be applied there).
- Deploying over a Blender that has loaded the DLLs: Windows keeps a loaded DLL locked, so a deploy that must replace
  one (a new libjxl version) needs every Blender of that target closed. Deploy copies changed files only, so later
  add-on versions with the same DLLs are not affected. Not tried.
- Which codestream level libjxl chose for the 856 Mpx file (over level 5's 2^28 px) and whether other decoders read
  it; libjxl itself decoded it.
- A 4.3 Gpx master; another machine.

### Proposals (not answered by the maintainer)

- **P57: the knob's size.** The knob's master shrinks by 29 % (the criterion asks 35 %); nothing in libjxl's lossless
  options reaches 35 % (32.6 % at effort 10 with predictor 15 and 1024 px groups, about 90 x slower). Options: (a) accept,
  as an object with more noise compresses less; (b) effort 9 with predictor 15 below a size (knob 32.3 %, panel
  41.6 %); (c) groups of 1024 px (panel 40.6 %, knob 29.9 %). Recommendation: (a), the large masters (43 to 50 %)
  are where the disk goes.
- **P58: Apply's time at 856 Mpx.** 1.15 x Session 24's figure, 1.05 x a same-day run of the PNG master. The decode
  is not the cost: the scratch files take 172 to 210 s for either format, mostly the `.ao.map` (21 MB of zlib, 3.4 GB of
  int32 codes tiled). Options: (a) accept, re-measure on an idle machine; (b) the map's codes as int16 or the map
  stored tiled (a format change); (c) P56. Recommendation: (a).
- **Apply of a set without the library** is refused as a whole when one of its masters is `.jxl` (an error per set,
  nothing written). The alternative is to leave those faces white and list them, as for a missing master; refusing was
  kept so that a partial texture is never written silently. For the maintainer to confirm.

  line). The objective is that the masters encode and decode losslessly. Session 26's rows 2 (at least 35 % smaller)

  results are measurements, not criteria. P57 and P58 are therefore withdrawn.

  another OS or a broken install).

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar + Claude (Opus 5.5, sub-agent executing Session 25's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** 06612fa (the work committed as 9de87fd, this session's text after it)

Session 25's hand-off was executed (decisions 34 to 36) on top of the JPEG XL masters, as `fss_blender_tools` 0.58.0
(0.56.0 and 0.57.0 were taken). Every Blender run went through the CPU cap (50 %), background, `--factory-startup`,
Cycles at 12 threads, one of ours at a time, started from `tests/_work/ao_bake/s30/` (the tool's second Blender
inherited the cap), each under a watchdog on time and memory (`s30/run_watch.ps1`); none fired. The production files
were not opened: the VS selector was baked on a copy of `s25/COCKPIT.blend` in `s30/cockpit/` (deleted afterwards).
Another session's capped jobs (a C++ build at 08:21, a benchmark at 08:23) ran on the machine during the last seconds
of the floor run and during the last full suite; the later suites waited for them to end. Times are therefore
measurements, not comparisons (decision 38).

### What Was Built

| File | What |
| :--- | :--- |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_store.py` | `mover_range` (the actions and unmuted NLA strips of the object and its animated parents), `pose_count`, `pose_frames` (N frames spread evenly, both ends included; none for N = 1 or without a range, with the reason), `occluder_mode`, `custom_occluders`; Refresh picks the occluders by mode (Scene rule as before, Self only: none, Custom: the object's list, its movers marked as posed) and adds to the surroundings hash only what differs from 0.57 (the mode when not the scene rule, the pose frames and the posed occluders when a mover has poses), so masters of one pose and the scene rule keep their hashes; the job carries the frames, the reason and the posed flags; `start_bake` hides the second Blender's console (`CREATE_NO_WINDOW`) when hidden and removes the old preview file |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_runner.py` | `pose_setup`: per pose frame the target's world matrix relative to the current frame (in the bake's space) and the posed occluders' meshes, the scene back at the current frame afterwards; a target whose evaluated vertices (object space) differ between poses is baked in the current frame and the reason logged and reported. Per tile every pose is baked before the next tile (target and pieces placed by the pose's matrix, the other poses' posed occluders hidden from rendering), each pose's red channel summed per pixel in the tile's own buffers (float64 and a count), the mean quantized once; pose p's seeds add `POSE_SEED` x p (7919). `status.json` gives the pose; the index entry `poses`, `n_poses`, `pose_note`, `occluder_mode`. The preview: written to `preview.bin` after each tile and composed strip (at most every 0.25 s) hidden or visible; with a window the 3D views become Image Editors. `status.json` and `index.json` replaced with retries (below) |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_core.py` | `POSE_SEED`, `pose_seed`; the preview file (`write_preview`, `read_preview`: a header and at most 256 x 256 px of 8-bit grey) |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake.py` | Scene settings Poses (default 5), Occluders (Scene Rule, Self Only), Run (Hidden, the default, or Visible); the object settings `fss_ao_bake` (Poses, 0 for the scene's; Occluders Default, Scene Rule, Self Only, Custom; the custom list) with the operator `fss.ao_bake_custom_occluders` (Add Selected, Remove, Clear) and an Object box in the panel for the active mesh (its range and pose count when it moves); "Pose p of N" while a mover bakes; the preview: the progress timer loads `preview.bin` into a `bpy.utils.previews` image, drawn with `template_icon`; the bake report lists a mover not baked in poses and why |
| `fss_blender_tools/__init__.py`, `README.md`, `tests/README.md` | 0.58.0; the section "AO Bake Options"; the suite row |
| `tests/test_ao_bake.py` | `test_custom_occluders` (row 4), `test_option_staleness` (row 5), `test_run_mode` (row 7; the visible run with `FSS_AO_BAKE_TEST_VISIBLE=1`), `test_poses` (row 1, the wheel; a deforming mover); `-- only=<tests>` runs single tests |
| `tests/bake_prototype/s30_identity.py`, `s30_vs_selector.py` | Row 3 on the suite's file (bake, hashes of decoded pixels and maps, compare two runs); rows 2 and 6 on the cockpit copy (bake at N poses, the profile per angle sector about the rotation axis read from the animation) |

How the poses are combined with the tiles (hand-off step 1): every pose of a tile before the next tile, summed in the
tile's own buffers (12 bytes per tile pixel more than one pose); the band, the composition and the files are as at one
pose, so memory does not grow with the master or with N. The scene is posed once per pose frame before the tiles, not
per tile. Time: the Cycles bakes N times (the wheel 2.37 s at 1 pose, 20.06 s at 8; the VS selector 27.8 s, 133.9 s
at 5 and 214.3 s at 8 poses), composition and files once.

**A fault found by the faster polling:** with the panel's timer reading `status.json` while the runner replaced it,
Windows refused the replace (`PermissionError`) and the master was reported failed (first full suite run, polling
every 0.1 s). The panel polls every 0.5 s, so 0.57 could meet it too, rarely. The runner now retries the replace for up
to 1 s; the preview file is simply written at the next update.

**A deviation from the hand-off:** a mover whose mesh deforms between poses is baked in the **current frame**, not the
first pose: the layout, the mesh hash and the master's triangles are the current frame's, and baking another frame's
shape into them would put the AO of one shape onto another. Said in `bake.log` and the bake report ("its mesh deforms
between poses (shape keys or an armature): baked in the current frame only"). For the maintainer to confirm.

### Not Verified

- The panel in an interactive Blender: the Object box, the custom list and its buttons, "Pose p of N" and the preview
  icon on screen (drawn only into the suite's recording layout; the preview's pixels and state read in background).
- The visible window's contents (3D views turned into Image Editors): the run finished, nobody looked at it. The
  hidden run started from an interactive Blender (that no console window flashes).
- A mover under a bone parent (its range from the armature's action), NLA strips as the range, scaled animation (the
  pose matrix then scales the target), a posed occluder that deforms (built per pose from its evaluated mesh, so it
  should follow; not tested).
- GPU, macOS, Linux; another machine.

### Proposals (not answered by the maintainer)

- **P59: a full turn counts its end twice.** On a full-turn animation (the VS selector, 0 to 100 = 0 to 360 degrees)
  the first and last poses are the same position, so 5 poses give 4 positions and leave four faint imprints (row 2:
  0.39 x; 8 poses 0.149 x). Options: (a) as now (decision 34: both ends included); (b) when the target's matrix at the
  range's end equals that at its start (within 1e-5), spread the N poses over the range without its end, so N
  positions; (c) a higher default (8). Recommendation: (b), one test in `pose_setup`; ranges that are not a full turn
  are unchanged.
- **P60: a mover's occluders come from its current-frame bounds.** Under the scene rule the occluders are the static
  meshes within the AO distance of the mover's bounds at the current frame; a part that travels farther than 0.1 m (a
  lever, a sliding cover) can miss occluders near its other poses. Options: (a) as now; (b) Refresh reads each
  mover's bounds at its pose frames (setting the frame in the artist's Blender, slower); (c) the runner adds the
  static meshes near the other poses (not in the hash). Recommendation: (a) until a case appears.
- **P61: a mover's master turns stale when the current frame changes,** also when baked in poses (its world matrix at
  the current frame is in the hash, as in 0.57), although the averaged AO does not depend on the frame. Options: (a)
  as now; (b) for a mover in poses, hash its matrix relative to the animated parent instead. Recommendation: (a), it
  is the 0.57 behaviour and costs only a re-bake.

## Session 31: Panel Fixes After the First Use of 0.58.0

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5

### The Maintainer's Test (0.58.0 deployed)

The maintainer re-baked the VS selector with Bake Selected (5 poses, 266 s) and applied it. Notes on the panel:

- The scene's Poses and Occluders read as the object's; the object box asked for poses again ("Poses (0: scene)")
  and for occluders again, which was confusing; with Self Only a pose count means nothing.
- The Apply box said "No texture in the ORM slot" for a material that has one. Found: the box showed the settings of
  the list's highlighted row, another material (only `MAT_STD_COCKPIT_INTERACTION` of the cockpit's sets has an ORM
  texture), and did not say which set it was for.
- The File field shows with the ORM target, and Apply was enabled with no valid target.
- Refresh was pressed expecting a re-bake: Refresh only compares the masters with the file; Bake Selected and Bake
  Missing and Stale bake.
- The interaction set's ORM texture is the real one; its O channel comes from this tool's bake, not from Substance.

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 40: the AO Bake panel's fixes** (maintainer, 20261113):
  - The scene settings are named **Default Poses** and **Default Occluders**; the run mode's row is labelled
    **Bake Window**.
  - The object box: **Occluders** offers Default (naming the scene's choice), Scene Rule, Self Only, Custom; for a
    mover, **Own Poses** with its count, or the default shown; hidden when the object's occluders are Self Only. A
    mover with Self Only is baked in one pose (nothing around it moves with it).
  - The texture-set list shows only the selected objects' sets, all when nothing is selected; the Apply box names the
    set it is for (the selection's only set, else the highlighted row).
  - Separate File shows the file name, resolution and layout; ORM Texture shows the slot's texture read only. The
    set's Apply is enabled only when its target can be written (an ORM texture whose file exists; an output folder
    that exists or can be made), and refused otherwise.
- Not taken now: a noise reduction of the masters (Blender's bake ignores Cycles' denoiser; OpenImageDenoise on the
  master could smear AO across island edges). It would be tried on a few masters first, as a separate decision.

### Built (0.59.0)

| File | What |
| :--- | :--- |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake.py` | The names; `object_occluder_items` (a dynamic enum with the static list's values, so stored choices stay); `own_poses` (a get/set view of `poses`, no new stored data); `selected_sets`, `apply_material`, `target_problem`; `FSS_UL_AOSets.filter_items`; the Apply box; `FSS_OT_AOApply` refuses a set whose target cannot be written |
| `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_store.py` | A mover with Self Only gets no poses (its key as a one-pose bake: a mover with Self Only baked by 0.58 at N poses becomes "Surroundings changed" once) |
| `tests/test_ao_bake.py` | `test_panel_selection`; `test_poses` asserts one pose for Self Only |
| `fss_blender_tools/README.md` | The options' names and the Apply box |

## Session 32: A Full Turn's Poses (P59) and Panel Labels

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** 6143fdb

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 41: when a mover's two range ends are the same pose, its N poses are spread over the range without its
  end** (maintainer, 20261113; P59). A full turn at 5 poses: frames 0, 20, 40, 60, 80 (72 degrees apart) instead of
  0, 25, 50, 75, 100, whose first and last are one position.
- **Decision 42: labels** (maintainer, 20261113; amends decision 40): the object's poses are **Poses: Default |
  Manual**, Manual showing the number; the run mode is **Run In: Background | Foreground** (stored values unchanged).
- **Working rule:** the AO bake suite (`test_ao_bake.py`, about 100 s) runs only for changes to the bake process, not
  for small panel changes.

### Built (0.60.0)

- `ao_bake_store.ends_coincide`: the transforms (`matrix_basis`) of the object and each animated parent at both ends,
  from their F-curves: the action's, or the one unmuted NLA strip's (time mapped by its start, scale and repeat, held
  outside it); an action with strips, or several strips, keeps both ends. `pose_frames` drops the end when they
  coincide. A first version read actions only and missed the VS selector, whose turn is an NLA strip (found by posing
  the copy at frames 0 and 100 with `frame_set`: its world matrix differs by 1.8e-7).
- The panel: `pose_mode` (a get/set view of `poses`); the run mode's labels.
- `test_poses`: a full turn (0 to 360 degrees over 0 to 100) at 4 poses gives 0, 25, 50, 75, as an action and as an
  NLA strip; the wheel's 0 to 315 degrees keeps both ends.

Movers with a full turn baked by 0.58 or 0.59 at several poses become "Surroundings changed" (their frames changed).

## Session 33: The Panel's Preview Shows the Object's Own Master

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** 4c48a97

### Found by the Maintainer (0.60.0)

With `LEVER_LANDINGGEAR` selected, the panel showed the VS selector's master and no bake buttons. Since 0.58.0 the
panel drew either the bake preview or the bake buttons; the preview stayed after a bake ended, so the buttons never
came back until Blender restarted or the file was opened again, and the picture belonged to the last bake, not to the
selected object.

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 43: the preview shows the master the object has** (maintainer, 20261113): while a bake runs, the master
  being baked; otherwise the active object's own master, in its Object box. The bake buttons are drawn whenever no
  bake runs.

### Built (0.60.1)

- The runner writes `<stem>.ao.thumb` (at most 256 px, the preview file's format) with each finished master; a
  cancelled master keeps its old thumbnail with its old files; renames move it (`core.all_master_files`).
- The panel: the live preview only while running; the bake buttons otherwise; in the Object box the active object's
  master ("Its master (as baked)"), "No master baked yet", or for a master of more than 64 Mpx without a thumbnail,
  "Bake Selected makes one". A timer (0.5 s, persistent, not in a background Blender) loads the thumbnail when the
  active object, its master or the thumbnail's time changes, never while drawing; a master baked before 0.60.1 gets
  its thumbnail made from the master on first selection (at most 64 Mpx). The stem comes from the last Refresh, else
  from `index.json` (the entry's object).
- `test_object_preview`: the bake buttons drawn after a bake; A's thumbnail from the bake, made again from the master
  when deleted; the preview follows the active object.

Verified: `test_ao_bake.py` ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (112.8 s), `test_panels.py` passes (logs in
`tests/_work/ao_bake/s34/`). Not verified: the panel in an interactive Blender (the timer and the icons; icon ids are
0 in a background Blender, so the test checks the loaded state).

## Session 35: Apply in Steps, with Progress and Cancel

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** 00f4bfe

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 45: Apply from the panel runs in steps inside the artist's Blender, with a progress box and Cancel**
  (maintainer, 20261113; chosen over a second Blender, which would read the saved file only). It reads the open
  file's UVs as before. While it runs the panel shows only its progress (texture set n of m, the master or the file
  being written, time spent and left) and Cancel; the set being applied keeps its file as it was, sets applied before
  stay written. One piece of work (decoding one master, a band of its copy, a strip of the file) cannot be split, so a
  very large master still holds Blender for its decode.

### Built (0.61.0)

- `ao_bake_apply.apply_set_steps` (a generator: yields per master, per band of a master's copy and per 256-row strip
  of the file; the file goes to a `.tmp` replaced at the end, so closing it early writes nothing) and `apply_steps`
  (several sets, progress as set index and fraction: the masters 90 %, the file 10 %); `apply_set` and `apply` run
  them to the end, so scripts and the tests get the same results as before.
- `FSS_OT_AOApply`: `invoke` (the panel) runs the steps on a 0.02 s timer, 0.1 s of work per tick; `execute`
  (scripts) in one go. `FSS_OT_AOApplyCancel`. Bake and Apply cannot start while Apply runs. A file load closes a
  running Apply.

Verified: `test_ao_bake.py` ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (104.8 s) with `test_apply_steps` (15 steps; the file equal to
Apply in one go; closed after 7 steps: the file untouched, no `.tmp`), `test_panels.py` passes; the panel drawn in the
applying state (a mock layout) shows only the progress and Cancel (`s35/draw_applying.py`). Not verified: the modal
operator in an interactive Blender (a background Blender has no window or timer events).

## Session 36: Soften Sharp Edges in Apply

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** f1a6d17

### What Was Found First

The maintainer saw a hard step in the AO at the rim of the knob cap's holes and asked whether Apply's sampling was
wrong. Measured on MESH_SHARED_ROTARY_CAP3 (`s29/bleed_truth.py`, 384,258 texels of the applied texture): the texture
is within 0.007 of the master everywhere, 0.007 also within half a texel of a sharp edge, so Apply does not spread AO
across edges; the master is within 0.012 to 0.027 of an independent ray trace (cosine hemisphere, the mesh alone, the
scene's distance), ray noise. A cross-section (`s29/cap3_section.py`) shows gradients on every face. The step at a rim
is the AO of a sharp convex edge: the surface beside the rim sees the whole hemisphere, the wall just inside it faces
across the hole. The AO distance does not cause it: on a small pocket the wall reads 0.12 at 100 mm and at 5 mm, 0.46
at 2 mm (`s29/cap3_rim.py`). Blender's bake is about 0.07 lighter than the ray trace on faces next to rounded edges
(it turns the hemisphere by the smoothed normals); not changed.

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 46: Soften Sharp Edges, in Apply** (maintainer, 20261113): an option (scene setting, off by default)
  that treats convex sharp edges as slightly rounded. Only the darker side changes: it brightens toward the brighter
  side's value at the edge, so the brighter side is never darkened and a sharp lip does not look blunt. Convex edges
  only: inside corners keep their contact shadow. The width is in millimetres and never less than one texel of the
  target (option C of the proposal). Done in Apply, not in the bake: the masters stay as baked and a new width needs
  only a new Apply. The averaging of shared meshes' copies (Average, Most Lit, Darkest, a scene default and an object
  override) is to follow, after this.

### Built (0.62.0)

- `ao_bake_core.soften_context`: per master, the welded edges of two faces whose normals differ by more than 30
  degrees (the copy's surface angle) and whose far corners each lie behind the other face; per triangle of the set
  its width (the setting, at least its own target texel) and the edges within reach (a KD-tree of points along the
  edges). `_soften`: each inside and padding pixel within the width of such an edge, on a face of either side's
  surface and on that side, is raised by (brighter edge value - darker edge value) x (1 - smoothstep(distance /
  width)), both edge values read from the master at the nearest point of the edge; near several edges the largest
  raise wins. `copy_master(..., soften=)` applies it; `info['softened_px']`.
- Apply: `soften_edges` and `soften_width` (default 0.3 mm, a starting value) in the scene's AO Bake settings, a row in
  the panel's Apply box; the report line "sharp edges softened (w mm, at least a texel): n px".

### Verified

- `test_ao_bake.py` ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (106.2 s) with the new `test_soften_edges`: a master built by hand (top
  1.0 over a convex lip to a wall 0.4, a concave corner to a floor 0.6, UVs continuous across both): one convex edge
  of two found; the wall equal to the smoothstep to 0.00000; the top, the floor and the wall at the concave corner
  unchanged; no pixel darker; a width under a texel softens one column (0.700 at its centre).
- The knob cap on a scratch copy (`s29/soft_knob.py`, Apply Selected of AUTOBRAKE_KNOB into a 4096 px file): off,
  0.3 mm and 1 mm in 21.6, 21.6 and 21.9 s; 12,774 and 36,719 pixels raised, none darker, the largest raise 0.72
  (wall pixels at the rim). Rendered with the AO as emission (`s29/cmp_bottom45.png`): the hole walls brighten up to
  the rim, the surface around unchanged; at 1 mm the small hexagon's faces are mostly within the width.
- Not verified: the panel row in an interactive Blender. The artist guide is deferred to the doc pass.

## Session 37: Shared Meshes Baked per Copy

- **Date:** 20261113
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** 081bfd9

### Maintainer Decisions

- **Decision 47: a shared mesh's copies are each baked in their own surroundings and combined** (maintainer,
  20261113; replaces "a shared mesh gets its self-shading only" of P35). One master per shared mesh as before. Each
  visible copy (the scene rules decide which objects are baked: hidden, excluded, LOD1+ and No Export copies take no
  part) is baked with its own occluder setting and list and, when it moves, its own poses; the copies are combined
  per master pixel: **Average** (the default), **Most Lit** (the lightest copy) or **Darkest**. A scene default
  (Shared Meshes) and an override that belongs to the mesh, shown in the Object box of any copy, so copies cannot
  disagree. Every shared master becomes "surroundings changed" once and needs one re-bake. Cost: one bake per copy
  and pose.

### Built (0.63.0)

- `ao_bake_store`: `surroundings(obj, mover)` (the single object's occluders, poses and key, now also used per copy);
  for a shared mesh `item['copies']` (per copy: object, occluder mode and names, poses, posed occluders) and
  `item['combine']` (`shared_combine`: the mesh's `fss_ao_shared.combine`, else the scene's `shared_combine`); the
  surroundings hash covers the combine and every copy's key. `make_job` passes the copies with their occluders'
  skip slots.
- `ao_bake_runner.setup_copies`: one view per copy and pose (the target placed at the copy relative to the first
  copy; a pose's placement composed on it; only that copy's occluders rendered; each occluder built once);
  `bake_tile_views`: per tile, a copy's views averaged, the copies combined (mean, maximum or minimum), a seed per
  view. The master's index entry records `combine` and `copies`. Objects without copies bake exactly as before.
- `ao_bake`: the scene's Shared Meshes beside Default Occluders; `Mesh.fss_ao_shared.combine` (Default (scene's
  choice), Average, Most Lit, Darkest) shown as "Shared mesh, N copies" in the Object box.

### Verified

- `test_ao_bake.py` ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (107.2 s). `test_bake_all`: the shared cubes S1/S2 now carry plane D's
  contact shadow (minimum 0.0; it was 1.0 at self-shading). New `test_shared_combine` (S1 and S2, 2 cm apart on
  plane D): the face between them (-X, seen from S1 at S2 and from S2 at open space) Darkest 0.295, Average 0.551,
  Most Lit 0.806, the average the mean of the two; the top 1.0 and the contact shadow in every mode; the override
  each way, back to the default and a moved copy each give "surroundings changed"; the entry records Average and 2
  copies.
- The knob cap on a scratch copy of the E2 cockpit (`s29/shared_knob.py`, one pose per copy, Average): 18 copies,
  16 to 43 occluders each; 18 views baked in 257.5 s (the self-only master: 25.1 s), 315.7 s in all. Applied into a
  4096 px file: the mean over the knob's pixels 0.849 (self-only) to 0.580, 359,453 pixels darker by more than 0.05,
  38 lighter, the largest darkening 0.563; renders in `s29/knob_shared_vs_self.png` (the sides and the base darker
  where the knobs sit on their panels). At the default 5 poses, a moving copy has 5 views: the bake time grows with
  copies x poses.
- `test_panels.py` passes. Not verified: the Object box row and the scene setting in an interactive Blender. The
  artist guide is deferred to the doc pass.

## Session 38: Custom Occluders with Add and Ignore

- **Date:** 20261115
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude
- **Base Commit SHA:** e4bf329, plus the OptiX baker's uncommitted work in `fss_blender_tools`

Decisions 48 to 59 were taken in sessions not yet written here (the journal catch-up the maintainer deferred to the
doc pass); this session's decision follows them.

### Problem

The maintainer could not use the Custom occluder list on the E2 copilot seat cushion (`SEAT_CUSHION_FUR_COPILOT`):
selecting another object switches the Object box to it (the box follows the active object; Add Selected adds the
selected objects but the active one, which the panel does not say), and Custom means "only the listed objects", so
excluding one neighbour from the scene rule needs every other object listed (1408 move against the cushion).

### Decisions

60. **Custom occluders: the scene as occluder, and a list of objects each added or ignored.** With Custom, the
    Object box shows a checkbox "Scene as occluder" (off: only the object and its added objects darken it; on: the
    scene rule, decision 15) and the list. Each item has its own state, switched by clicking its icon: Add (green
    plus: the object darkens it) or Ignore (red cross, the row in red: the object never darkens it). The state does
    not change meaning when the checkbox changes; items without effect are greyed (Ignore with the scene off; Add
    with the scene on for an object the scene rule already includes). An object field with an eyedropper adds an
    object without changing the selection; Add Selected stays (the selected objects but the active one). A Custom
    list saved before becomes "Scene as occluder" off with every item Add, and bakes as before (its masters stay up
    to date).

### Hand-Off: Custom Occluders with Add and Ignore

Start only when the OptiX baker's changes to `ao_bake.py`, `ao_bake_store.py` and `ao_bake_runner.py` are committed
(they are uncommitted in the working tree as this is written).

Read first: decision 60 above, decisions 15 and 35 in this journal, `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake.py`
(`FSS_AOObjectSettings`, `FSS_OT_AOCustomOccluders`, `FSS_UL_AOOccluders`, the Object box),
`fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_store.py` (`occluder_mode`, `custom_occluders`, `surroundings`), `tests/test_ao_bake.py`.

Steps:

1. Settings: `FSS_AOObjectSettings.custom_scene` (Bool, "Scene as occluder", default off);
   `FSS_AOOccluderRef.ignore` (Bool, default off, so an existing list reads as all Add).
2. Store: with Custom, the occluders are (the scene rule's objects for this object, as with Scene Rule, when
   `custom_scene` is on, else none) minus the ignored ones plus the added ones. The surroundings key of a Custom
   object with the checkbox off and no ignored item stays exactly as today, so saved masters stay up to date; any
   other combination changes the key as needed.
3. Panel: the checkbox; the list rows with the icon toggle (an operator or a property drawn as an icon), Ignore rows
   in red (`alert`), items without effect greyed (`active = False`); an object field with the eyedropper that
   appends the picked object (not the object itself, no duplicates); Add Selected, Remove, Clear as today. A line
   that says how Add Selected works ("the selected objects but the active one").
4. Tests in `test_ao_bake.py`: the four combinations (scene off/on x Add/Ignore) give the expected occluder names;
   an existing Custom list (no new properties set) gives the same surroundings key and stays up to date; an ignored
   occluder's shadow is gone from the baked master (a contact shadow from a neighbour, scene on, the neighbour
   ignored); the panel draws without writing data (`test_panels.py`).
5. Raise `fss_blender_tools`' version; append the session that built it (Built, finish lines with numbers, Not
   Verified).

Finish lines: step 4's tests pass; `test_ao_bake.py` and `test_panels.py` pass as a whole; a master of an existing
Custom list keeps its hash.

Do not: run C++ builds; run Blender other than in background under
`C:\Users\uray\.claude\tools\cpucap\cpucap.exe 50`, one at a time (the maintainer's own Blenders run interactively and
are never touched); write into an aircraft repository; commit, push or deploy.

## Session 41: The Detected Tree

- **Date:** 20261115
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude
- **Base Commit SHA:** 1bc86fa, plus Sessions 39 and 40 (uncommitted)

### Decisions

63. **The Detected list is a tree with the detected objects' parents, joined for several selected objects.** Asked
    by the maintainer: the flat list of the cockpit was too long to work through, and a whole branch should be
    ignored or added through its parent. Each row: [+] and [-] showing what the row does (lit [+]: it darkens the
    object; lit red [-]: it never does; both unlit: mixed, some do and some do not), the fold arrow of a parent row
    (folded at first; the name filter opens the branches), and the name, which selects that object alone and makes
    it active (the panel then shows it; the maintainer accepted losing the previous selection). A parent row shows
    the number of detected objects in its branch. With several meshes selected the tree joins their detected
    occluders and each row shows how many of them detect it (n/m); the Custom list above stays the active object's.
    Clicking the unlit button applies to each tree object that detects something in the row's branch: Scene Rule
    becomes Custom with Scene as occluder on [-], Self Only becomes Custom without the scene on [+]; the items inside
    the branch are removed, and one item on the row is added when the parents' items do not already give the wanted
    state. A Custom item has With its children: it then covers the object's children at every depth as the hierarchy
    is when baked; an item nearer to an object wins. An empty or another non-mesh parent can be listed (eyedropper,
    Add Selected) and goes in with With its children on.

### Built (`fss_blender_tools` 0.80.0)

- `ao_bake_store.py`: `custom_refs` (the list by name: ignore, with its children), `custom_state` (the object's own
  item, else the nearest parent's item with its children) and `custom_occluders` resolving through them; a list
  without the new flag gives the same occluders, so its masters stay up to date.
- `ao_bake.py`: `FSS_AOOccluderRef.children`; the eyedropper and Add Selected accept a parent; the list's second icon
  (action CHILDREN); `tree_targets`, `detected_tree` (cached per targets and Refresh), `tree_rows`, `row_state`,
  `set_row`; the operators `fss.ao_bake_tree_set`, `fss.ao_bake_tree_fold`, `fss.ao_bake_tree_select`;
  `FSS_UL_AODetected` filtering and ordering the scene's objects as the tree. The action IGNORE of decision 62 is
  replaced by [+] and [-].

### Verified

`test_ao_bake.py` ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (163.6 s). `test_detected` now uses [+] and [-]: O1's row lit [+]; [-]
makes A Custom with Scene as occluder and O1 ignored (its occluders [], surroundings changed), the row lit [-]; [-]
again changes nothing; [+] removes the item (occluders ['O1']); without the scene the row is lit [-]. The new
`test_tree` (empty TP made the parent of O1 and S1; every master stays up to date): A and D joined give the rows
['S2', 'TP'], unfolded ['S2', 'TP', 'O1', 'S1'], the filter 's1' ['TP', 'S1'], TP counted 2/2; [-] on TP makes both
Custom with Scene as occluder and TP ignored with its children (A [], D without S1); [+] on O1 adds it back for A
alone, TP mixed; [+] on TP removes the branch's items; Self Only with [+] on S2 gives D ['S2']; TP added with its
children (scene off) gives ['O1', 'S1'], without the flag [], S1 ignored on its own ['O1']; Add Selected and the
eyedropper add the empty with its children; the name selects S2 alone and makes it active; the rows draw without

## Session 42: Oriented Boxes in the Scene Rule

- **Date:** 20261115
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude
- **Base Commit SHA:** 1bc86fa, plus Sessions 39 to 41 (uncommitted)

### Decisions

64. **The scene rule measures the gap between the objects' own boxes, turned with them, not only their world
    boxes; an occluder farther than the AO distance is dropped.** Asked by the maintainer after the armrest listed
    screws far below it; a surface-to-surface check was not wanted. The world boxes' gap is tested first (as decision
    15); for the pairs left, the gap of the two objects' own boxes (their bounds in object space, as Blender's Bounds
    display shows them, placed in the world) along the 15 axes that can part two boxes; it is never more than the true
    distance, so no object within reach is dropped. It applies when neither object moves, or at the current frame for
    an object baked in one pose; a moving object keeps its world box swept over its poses (decision 59). An object
    whose axes are not square (a shear from a parent's uneven scale) keeps its world box. The bake loads only the
    listed occluders and its rays stop at the AO distance, so the dropped objects never darkened anything; masters
    whose list shrinks are marked "surroundings changed" once.

### Built (`fss_blender_tools` 0.81.0)

- `ao_bake_store.py`: `_oriented_box`, `oriented_gaps` (vectorized over the occluders), used in `refresh`'s `near()`
  after the world-box test for the still pairs.

### Verified

- `test_ao_bake.py` ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (167.2 s) with the new `test_oriented_rule`: a bar 0.8 m x 2 cm turned
  45 degrees beside A (AO distance 0.100 m), world box gap 0.010, own box gap 0.414: not an occluder of A, A up to
  date; turned towards A it is one (surroundings changed); aligned boxes give the world boxes' gap (1.000); a child
  of an unevenly scaled parent has no own box; removed, A is up to date. The movers' tests (poses, the wheel) pass
  unchanged. `test_panels.py` passes. The gap checked outside Blender on 200 random pairs of turned boxes: never more
  than the sampled distance between them.
- The E2 cockpit copy (`tests/_work/localview/COCKPIT.blend`, not saved): Refresh 6.63 s with world boxes only,
  5.49 s and 4.54 s with own boxes; occluder pairs 131746 to 128542; 208 of 570 objects get a shorter list, none a
  longer one. `SEAT_ARMREST_LT_COPILOT` keeps its 1104: it moves (the seat slides 329 mm over frames 0 to 100), so
  its list comes from its box swept over the motion (232 x 799 x 467 mm against 169 x 119 x 456 mm at frame 99).

Not verified: a re-bake of the cockpit (the AO of a master whose list shrank should be unchanged).

## Session 43: Own Boxes per Pose; the Tree's Select Button

- **Date:** 20261115
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude
- **Base Commit SHA:** 1bc86fa, plus Sessions 39 to 42 (uncommitted)

### Decisions

65. **A moving object's occluders are measured with the own boxes at each sampled frame.** Asked by the maintainer
    after Session 42 left the armrest at 1104 occluders: it slides with the seat, so its world box swept over the
    motion decided alone. The world boxes' test (swept for a mover) comes first; for the pairs left, both objects'
    own boxes are placed at each frame `motion()` sampled (the current frame included) and the smallest gap counts:
    an occluder is dropped only when it is beyond the AO distance at every sampled frame. Pairs where neither moves
    are measured once.

    selected the object; it should highlight the row as any Blender list does, and a button beside [+] and [-]

## Session 47: A Distance Column, a Flat View, Empties Folded Away

- **Date:** 20261115

- **Base Commit SHA:** 1bc86fa, plus Sessions 39 to 46 (uncommitted)

### Decisions

70. **The Detected list gets a distance column and a Tree or Flat toggle; the tree shows an empty parent only when it
    holds two objects or more that are not empties.** Asked by the maintainer. The distance is the scene rule's gap
    (the larger of the world boxes' gap and the own boxes' gap, the smallest over the poses; 0 where the boxes touch),
    in mm; a parent row shows the smallest in its branch, joined the smallest over the selected objects. The flat view
    lists the detected objects alone, nearest first. An empty whose branch holds fewer than two such objects is no row;
    its children move up to the row above it. Read as the branch, not the direct children: an empty over empties that
    hold the meshes stays a row (proposed by Claude, not yet confirmed).

### Built (`fss_blender_tools` 0.86.0)

- `ao_bake_store.py`: `near()` puts each occluder's gap in a dict when given one; Refresh and Find Occluders keep
  them in `_state['scene_gap']`.
- `ao_bake.py`: `detected_tree` folds the empties away and gives `gap`, `own_gap` and `detected`; `tree_rows` has the
  flat order; the list draws the distance column; the toggle is `detected_view` on the window manager's state, beside
  the refresh button.

### Verified

On a copy of today's cockpit (frame 0, 250 mm), Find Occluders for the armrest: 68 detected, every one with a gap from
0 to 250 mm; 17 of the 24 empties above them stay rows, each holding two objects or more; the flat view lists the 68,
nearest first. The armrest and the cushion joined: 597 detected, 85 of 408 empties stay. `test_panels.py` passes.
`test_tree` extended for the gaps, the flat order and an empty over one object (not run: it needs the AO Bake suite's
bakes, which the maintainer asked not to run for panel changes). Not verified: the list in an interactive Blender.

## Session 50: Apply Despite a Changed Mesh, a Leaner Panel

- **Date:** 20261115
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude
- **Base Commit SHA:** 1bc86fa, plus Sessions 39 to 49 (uncommitted)

### What Happened

The maintainer baked 7 objects and applied: the yoke `@MERGE_YOKE_AILERON_R` stayed white. Its master was recorded
with the baker's mesh hash (`3121b…`), while the open Blender computed `41d2c…` at the bake and at Apply, so Apply
judged the mesh changed and wrote white. The file itself gives `3121b…` in a background and in a UI Blender at every
frame; after the maintainer saved and reopened, Mesh changed read 0. The open session held stale data for that mesh;
its cause is not known. 1 of 77 masters had the two hashes differ.

### Decisions

73. **Apply copies a master whose mesh changed since its bake, and says so on its button.** The maintainer: Apply
    goes ahead; a stale mesh or UV is not the baker's responsibility. Apply Selected turns red with "N mesh changed"
    (the selection's masters graded so at the last Refresh). White remains only where there is no master, no
    curvature baked, or fewer triangles than baked (the master cannot be placed: "triangles changed").
74. **The panel shows less.** The ORM lines on which channel is written and what an export from another tool does
    are removed (the 8-bit line stays: "8-bit file: written as 16-bit"); Substance is never named. The Apply report
    leaves the panel (only a failed set and Cancel stay; the whole report goes to the console); the bake's result is
    one line, `done x of y, in z s, <date and time>`, plus a line per failed object, the rest in the console.

### Built (`fss_blender_tools` 0.89.0)

- `ao_bake_apply.py`: the mesh hash no longer blocks a copy (`mesh_changed` in the report); a master whose triangle
  list exceeds the mesh is white; the Substance warning removed.
- `ao_bake.py`: the Apply button's warning; the ORM lines; `_done` and `finish_bake` keep the panel to the lines
  above and print the rest.

### Verified

On a copy of the cockpit with the yoke's master copied from the E2 bake folder and the ORM texture copied to a scratch
folder (E2's file unchanged): a moved vertex grades Mesh changed, the button counts 1; Apply copies the yoke
(1,440,520 red pixels changed), reports it as mesh changed, nothing white, the panel's report empty; with half the
faces deleted the yoke is white, "triangles changed". The bake result line from a fake run: `done 5 of 5, in 54.5 s,
2026-11-15 18:10` and the failed object. `test_panels.py` passes; `test_ao_bake.py` updated (the report and Substance
asserts), not run.

## Session 54: Apply Rasterizes the Other Objects Only Near the Applied Ones

- **Date:** 20261115
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude
- **Base Commit SHA:** 1bc86fa, plus Sessions 39 to 53 (uncommitted)

### What Happened

The maintainer saw Apply go through every object of the texture set (66) when Run applied one object. Apply reads
every object of the set so that the applied objects' padding never goes onto another object's face; it rasterized each
other object over its whole UV crop. Profiled on a copy (one object applied into a 4096 px ORM copy): 16.8 s, of it 9.1
s rasterizing the other objects and 8.2 s compressing the PNG (zlib level 6, the file written whole).

### Built (`fss_blender_tools` 0.90.3, a fix, no decision)

- `ao_bake_apply.py`: with the selection applied, the applied objects' crops are found first; another object of the
  set is still read, but rasterized only where its crop overlaps one of them (the padding goes nowhere else).
  A first try, skipping objects by their UV bounds read from the mesh, was dropped: it skipped 8 of 65 (most objects
  carry an Auto Smooth node group or Mirror, whose UVs the mesh does not give).

### Verified

The same copy, the ORM texture copied to a scratch folder (E2's file not written): one object 16.8 s to 9.4 s, two
objects (the steer disengage and `@MERGE_YOKE_AILERON_R`) 49.5 s to 41.3 s; the written file byte-identical to the
old way's in both (the old way reproduced by a whole-crop overlap). `test_panels.py` passes; the AO Bake suite not run.
Left: the PNG compression, about 8 s per 4096 px file.

## Session 55: One Instance Bakes the Selected Copy (Decision 77)

- **Date:** 20261116
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** aee2a44, plus the uncommitted working tree (`fss_blender_tools` 0.96.0)

### What Happened

The maintainer, trying 0.96.0 on the E2 cockpit, selected `SHAKER_CUTOUT_2` (mesh `MESH_KEY_BTN_CAP_SQ_BAR`, 37
copies, Shared Meshes One Instance), whose parent is animated, and found no motion line and no Poses row. One Instance
bakes the mesh's picked copy, else its first copy by name (`ADS_HEATER`), and the panel shows the motion line and the
Poses row only on the copy that is baked (`_motion_info`); the master in E2's bake folder was baked from `ADS_HEATER`
in one pose.

### Maintainer Decision

77. **One Instance bakes the selected copy.** The maintainer: there is no need to pick a copy; the object selected is
    the one to bake. A bake with a copy of the mesh selected bakes that copy; with several copies of it selected, the
    panel offers a drop-down list of those copies to choose from (never an object picker: the artist does not know
    where the objects are). The master records the copy it was baked from in the bake folder's index (a bake does not
    write into the `.blend`), and Refresh grades it against that copy's surroundings and poses, so a later selection
    changes nothing. A bake without a copy selected (Bake All, Missing, Stale) keeps the copy the master was baked
    from; a mesh never baked takes its first copy by name. The Copy picker leaves the panel; the panel says which copy
    the master was baked from, and that copy shows the motion line and the Poses row.

## Session 56: Hand-Off, One Instance Bakes the Selected Copy (Decision 77)

- **Date:** 20261117
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5 (the hand-off; executed by a sub-agent, which appends Session 57)
- **Base Commit SHA:** e8222cb (`fss_blender_tools` 0.99.0), plus another session's uncommitted UV packer work in the
  same tree (not this task's; see Do Not)

### Why

Decision 77 (Session 55): One Instance bakes the copy the artist has selected, the master remembers that copy in the
bake folder's index, and Refresh grades against it. Today (decision 48) One Instance bakes the mesh's picked copy
(`fss_ao_shared.instance`, the Copy picker), else its first copy by name, so a selected copy whose parent moves
(`SHAKER_CUTOUT_2`) shows no motion line and is never baked.

### The Rule

1. **The copy Refresh grades against** (`store.shared_instance`, called in `refresh` for a mesh whose combine is
   `'ONE'`), first that applies:
   1. the bake's own choice from the selection (item 2), when Refresh is called by a bake;
   2. the index entry's new `instance` field (the copy's object name), when that object is still one of the copies;
   3. an index entry without `instance` (a master baked before this change): the mesh's old `fss_ao_shared.instance`
      pointer when it is one of the copies, so such masters keep their grade;
   4. the first copy by name (a mesh never baked, or its copy gone or renamed).
2. **The bake's choice** (`FSS_OT_AOBakeStart.execute`, scopes `AUTO` (Bake Selected) and `SELECTED` only; `STALE`
   (Missing and Stale) makes none): for each One Instance mesh with at least one selected copy, the copy named by the
   mesh's drop-down (item 3) when it is a selected copy, else the active object when it is a selected copy, else the
   first selected copy by name. Passed to `store.refresh(context, instances={mesh name: object name})`, which uses it
   in place of rule 1.2 to 1.4 for those meshes only. A changed copy changes the surroundings hash, so Bake Selected
   finds the master stale and bakes it; the same copy, up to date, is baked in full as today.
3. **The drop-down:** shown in the Object box under the Shared mesh row only when the active object's mesh is One
   Instance and two or more of its copies are selected: an enum whose items are those selected copies by name (no
   object picker), stored by name on `fss_ao_shared` (a hidden `StringProperty`, the enum's get and set mapping to it;
   a dynamic enum's index alone is not stable). Its shown value is the copy rule 2 would take.
4. **The index:** the runner writes `instance` (the baked copy's name, `item['copies'][0]['object']`) into a One
   Instance master's entry; other entries do not get the field. The job item already carries the copy.
5. **The panel:** the Copy picker (`instance`) and the "(first by name)" line leave the Object box. One line says
   which copy the master was baked from, from the last Refresh: `One Instance: baked from <copy>` when the master has
   an entry, `One Instance: <copy> (first by name)` when it has none; when the selection would bake another copy, a
   second line `Bake Selected bakes <copy>`. `_motion_info` stays as it is: the copy in the item's `copies` (now the
   remembered one) shows the motion line and the Poses row.
6. **Removed:** `_combine_update` (choosing One Instance set the pointer). The `instance` pointer stays registered,
   not drawn, for rule 1.3; its description says so. The `shared_combine` One Instance tooltip describes the new
   rule.

A relinked master (its mesh renamed, `refresh`'s relink) takes rule 1.3 or 1.4 on that Refresh, as the entry is found
after the items are graded; report whether it then grades Surroundings, do not build around it.

### Read First

- This journal's Sessions 37 (decision 47) and 55 (decision 77); AGENTS.md.
- `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_store.py`: `shared_combine`, `shared_instance`, `refresh` (the shared-mesh loop, the
  relink), `make_job`, `items_for_objects`.
- `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake.py`: `FSS_AOMeshSettings`, `_combine_update`, `_is_copy`, `SHARED_COMBINE_NAMES`, the
  `shared_combine` enum, `_motion_info`, `_draw_object`, `FSS_OT_AOBakeStart`.
- `fss_blender_tools/ao_bake_runner.py`: the index entry (`entry = dict(...)`, near line 1032).
- `tests/test_ao_bake.py` (`test_shared_combine`, the One Instance part from `# decision 48`), `tests/test_panels.py`,
  `tests/README.md`.

### Steps

1. Build the rule (items 1 to 6).
2. Tests in `test_shared_combine` (replace the decision 48 part), each on the two-copy scene there:
   1. S2 selected, Bake Selected: the entry's `instance` is `S2`, the faces as Most Lit's (as today's `ONE S2`).
   2. Then S1 selected, Refresh: S1's grade stays `UP_TO_DATE`, the item's copy `S2` (a selection changes nothing).
   3. S2 moved, Missing and Stale with S1 selected: the entry's `instance` stays `S2`.
   4. S1 and S2 selected, S2 active, the drop-down set to S1, Bake Selected: `instance` `S1`, the faces as Darkest's;
      then the drop-down unset (or naming an unselected copy) with S2 active: S2 is baked.
   5. The `instance` field deleted from the entry (as an old master) with the pointer on S2 and the master baked from
      S2: Refresh grades `UP_TO_DATE`; with the pointer empty, the first by name (S1) and `SURROUNDINGS`.
   6. A mesh never baked: the first copy by name.
   7. The remembered copy renamed: the first by name.
3. `tests/test_panels.py`: the Object box draws no `instance` property; the drop-down only with two or more copies of
   a One Instance mesh selected, its items those copies; the "baked from" line.
4. The suites: the whole `tests/test_ao_bake.py`, `tests/test_panels.py`, `tests/test_ao_bake_native_job.py`.
5. Append Session 57 to this journal: what was built, each finish line with its numbers (MET or NOT MET), what was not
   verified. Do not commit.

### Finish Lines

| # | Line | How it is checked |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Bake Selected with one copy selected bakes that copy; the index records it | step 2.1 |
| 2 | With several copies selected, the drop-down's copy is baked, else the active one | step 2.4 |
| 3 | A later selection, and Missing and Stale, leave the remembered copy and the grade unchanged | steps 2.2, 2.3 |
| 4 | A master baked before this change keeps its grade | step 2.5 |
| 5 | No Copy picker in the panel; the drop-down only with two or more selected copies; the "baked from" line | step 3 |
| 6 | The suites of step 4 pass | their last lines |

### Do Not

- Do not change the master format (beyond the new `instance` field), the bake, Apply, the scene rule, the other
  combines or `bl_info["version"]`.
- Another session's UV packer work is uncommitted in this tree: do not edit, revert, format or stage
  `fss_blender_tools/__init__.py`, `uv_pack.py`, `uv_align.py`, `uv_align_draw.py`, `tests/test_uv_pack.py` or
  `docs/journal/20261116.native-uv-packer.md`. Do not run `git checkout`, `git stash`, `git reset` or `git add`.
- Do not commit, push or deploy. The reviewing session does that.
- Never write into the aircraft repositories (`G:\e-jets-e2`, `F:\new-fss-aircraft-ejet`), the real bake folder
  `G:\e-jets-e2\assets\visual\cockpit\COCKPIT.bake`, or their textures; never write into `F:\glider`. Download nothing.
- Never stop or touch a running `blender.exe` you did not start (the maintainer's, the other session's). Before each
  run wait until `tasklist | grep -i blender.exe` is empty; run one Blender of your own at a time.
- **CPU rule (the machine crashes otherwise):** every test run goes through
  `C:\Users\uray\.claude\tools\cpucap\cpucap.exe 50 <command>`, one heavy job at a time; a C++ build (none is needed
  here) would be `C:\Users\uray\.claude\tools\cpucap\cpucap.exe 50 cmd /c native\ao_copy\build.cmd`, at most 12
  compiler threads, never `/MP` without a number, never `/affinity`, never a build and a test run at the same time.
- Background Blender only:
  `C:\Users\uray\.claude\tools\cpucap\cpucap.exe 50 G:\e-jets-e2\tools\blender\blender.exe -b --factory-startup --python tests\<suite>.py`
  (the suites purge the deployed add-on copy and import this repository's). From Git Bash write `cmd //c`.

Session 57: decision 77 as above; then the review.

## Session 57: One Instance Bakes the Selected Copy (Decision 77), Built

- **Date:** 20261117
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5 (a Claude sub-agent executing Session 56's hand-off)
- **Base Commit SHA:** e8222cb (`fss_blender_tools` 0.99.0, version not raised), plus the other session's uncommitted
  UV packer work (not touched)

### Built

- `ao_bake_store.py`: `shared_instance(mesh, copies, entry, chosen)` returns the copy and its source by rule 1 of
  Session 56: `bake` (the bake's choice), `entry` (the entry's `instance`), `pointer` (an entry without `instance`:
  the old picked copy), `first` (the first by name). `refresh(context, relink=True, instances=None)` reads the master's
  entry before grading and stores on a One Instance item `instance`, `instance_from` and `baked_from` (the copy the
  master was baked from: the entry's `instance`, for an old entry the pointer or the first by name; None without an

  other entries do not get the field.
- `ao_bake.py`: `FSS_OT_AOBakeStart.execute` passes `instances=bake_instances(context)` to Refresh for the scopes
  `AUTO` and `SELECTED` (Run uses `AUTO`); `STALE` passes none. `FSS_AOMeshSettings`: `_combine_update` removed;

## Session 60: Padding Beyond a Sharp Fold Continues Its Own Face (Decision 78)

- **Date:** 20261117
- **Developer:** uraymeiviar, Claude Opus 5.5
- **Base Commit SHA:** e8222cb, plus Sessions 56 to 59 (uncommitted); `fss_blender_tools` raised to **0.103.0**

### What Happened

The maintainer showed a jagged line of light and dark dashes along the rim of a hole in a panel (the hole's wall in
its own UV island, the panel's top in another). The wall's padding past its slanted UV edge was pale: Apply's padding
(`copy_master`) read the face across the nearest 3D edge, hinged flat beside the pixel's face, so past the rim it took
the bright top's AO. Texture filtering blends each edge texel with the padding beside it, and along the edge's
pixel staircase the share of the top's value changes from step to step: the dashes. Antialiasing at the edge would
put the same blend into the edge texels; the padding has to hold the wall's own AO.

### Maintainer Decision

78. **Padding beyond a sharp fold continues its own face.** A padding pixel beyond an edge where the two faces' normals
    differ by more than 30 degrees (the copy's surface angle, `SURFACE_DEG`) reads its own face, clamped onto it; below
    30 degrees (a smooth UV seam, the same surface going on) it still reads the neighbour hinged flat across the edge.

### Built

- `ao_bake_core.py`: `PADDING_FOLD_COS = SURFACE_COS`; `copy_master(..., fold_cos=None)` marks a padding pixel whose
  neighbour across its nearest edge folds by more (the dot product of the two face normals under the cosine) as its
  own face's; `info['padding_at_folds']` counts them. No C++ change: the padding read is the same, only its source
  triangle.
- Tests: the reference values (`tests/data/ao_native_reference.json`, locked) were recorded with the padding hinged
  across every edge. `tests/test_ao_copy_native.py` sets `core.PADDING_FOLD_COS = -2` (the old rule) for its
  recorded cases and skips `padding_at_folds` in the compared info; `tests/test_ao_bake.py` runs
  `test_soften_edges`, `test_native_copy`, `test_native_open` and `test_native_png` (their recorded values) under
  the old rule (`@recorded_padding`). New `test_fold_padding` in the harness: a wall folded 90 degrees into a top,
  and a wall bent 10 degrees into its continuation, each face in its own island.

### Re-Run

Each in the background under `cpucap.exe 50`, one at a time (logs in `tests/_work/fold_pad/`).

| Suite | Last lines |
| :--- | :--- |
| `test_ao_copy_native.py` | `PASS: test_fold_padding (90 degree fold: 504 wall and 504 top padding px within 0.00000 of their own face, 237 px at folds; the old rule gave 134 wall padding px the top's AO; the 10 degree seam unchanged)`; `590 cases equal`; `ALL AO COPY NATIVE TESTS PASSED (57.5 s)` |
| `test_ao_bake.py` | `reference values (160 cases equal ...)`, `scene rule reference (196 Refreshes ...)`, `ALL AO BAKE TESTS PASSED (201.0 s)` |
| `test_ao_bake_native.py` | `26 passed, 0 failed` |

Deployed with `tools\deploy.bat -Force -Addons fss_blender_tools` to E1, E2 and the maintainer's Blender 5.2.
